Based on my exploration of the codebase, I now have a good understanding of what we're working on. Let me summarize my findings:

## Summary of the Advanced Trading System

The system appears to be a sophisticated algorithmic trading platform called "Instinct AI Trading System" with various components:

1. **Core Trading Components**:
   - Strategy management (strategy_manager.py)
   - Various execution scripts for different strategies (run_*.py files)
   - Backtesting framework with walk-forward analysis capabilities
   - Risk management systems

2. **Specialized Modules**:
   - Market Microstructure Analysis: For analyzing market behavior at a granular level
   - Various Trading Strategies: Including LSTM-based, statistical arbitrage, and funding arbitrage
   - Models: ML-based components for prediction and analysis
   - Observability: End-to-end monitoring and metrics collection

3. **Utilities**:
   - Technical indicators
   - Signal processing
   - Portfolio allocation
   - Risk management tools
   - Performance analytics

4. **Documentation**:
   - Extensive documentation covering different aspects of the system
   - Implementation plans and guides
   - System architecture documentation

## Observability System

We were specifically working on rebuilding the End-to-End Observability System, which includes:

1. **Central Manager**: The `ObservabilityManager` class that coordinates all observability functions
2. **Metrics Collection**: For tracking performance indicators and system health
3. **Distributed Tracing**: For following operations through the system
4. **Structured Logging**: For detailed contextual logs

The observability system appears to be well-designed with integration for:
- OpenTelemetry and Prometheus for metrics
- Tracing with Jaeger/Zipkin integration
- Structured logging with different output formats

It includes specialized components for:
- `StrategyMetrics`: Tracking the performance of trading strategies
- `ExchangeTracer`: Monitoring exchange API interactions and order lifecycle

We've already implemented several key components including:
- Main observability manager
- Strategy metrics module
- Exchange tracer
- Configuration system
- Basic usage examples

What would you like to focus on next to continue our work on the observability system?

```diff

+ """
+ Exchange-Specific Optimization
+ -----------------------------
+ Provides exchange-specific optimizations to improve execution quality, reduce trading costs,
+ and leverage unique features of different cryptocurrency exchanges.
+ """
+ 
+ import time
+ import logging
+ from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any
+ 
+ # Import sub-modules
+ from .profiles.exchange_profiler import ExchangeProfiler
+ from .order_types.order_optimizer import OrderTypeOptimizer
+ from .routers.smart_order_router import SmartOrderRouter
+ from .analytics.exchange_analytics import ExchangeAnalytics
+ 
+ # Version information
+ __version__ = "0.1.0"
+ __author__ = "Instinct AI Trading"
+ 
+ # Global instance
+ _exchange_optimizer = None
+ 
+ # Configure logging
+ logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
+ 
+ def setup_exchange_optimization(
+     observability_manager=None,
+     enable_latency_tracking: bool = True,
+     default_exchanges: List[str] = None,
+     router_config: Dict[str, Any] = None
+ ) -> "SmartOrderRouter":
+     """
+     Initialize the exchange optimization system.
+     
+     Args:
+         observability_manager: Optional observability manager for metrics and tracing
+         enable_latency_tracking: Whether to track latency for exchange operations
+         default_exchanges: List of default exchanges to include
+         router_config: Configuration for the smart order router
+         
+     Returns:
+         Configured SmartOrderRouter instance
+     """
+     global _exchange_optimizer
+     
+     logger.info("Initializing Exchange Optimization System")
+     
+     # Create exchange profiler
+     exchange_profiler = ExchangeProfiler(
+         observability_manager=observability_manager,
+         enable_latency_tracking=enable_latency_tracking
+     )
+     
+     # Add default exchanges if provided
+     if default_exchanges:
+         for exchange in default_exchanges:
+             exchange_profiler.register_exchange(exchange)
+     
+     # Create order type optimizer
+     order_optimizer = OrderTypeOptimizer(
+         exchange_profiler=exchange_profiler,
+         observability_manager=observability_manager
+     )
+     
+     # Create exchange analytics
+     exchange_analytics = ExchangeAnalytics(
+         observability_manager=observability_manager
+     )
+     
+     # Create smart order router
+     router_config = router_config or {}
+     _exchange_optimizer = SmartOrderRouter(
+         exchange_profiler=exchange_profiler,
+         order_optimizer=order_optimizer,
+         exchange_analytics=exchange_analytics,
+         observability_manager=observability_manager,
+         **router_config
+     )
+     
+     logger.info("Exchange Optimization System initialized successfully")
+     return _exchange_optimizer
+ 
+ def get_exchange_optimizer() -> "SmartOrderRouter":
+     """
+     Get the global exchange optimizer instance.
+     
+     If the exchange optimizer has not been set up yet, it will be initialized
+     with default settings.
+     
+     Returns:
+         SmartOrderRouter instance
+     """
+     global _exchange_optimizer
+     
+     if _exchange_optimizer is None:
+         logger.info("Auto-initializing Exchange Optimization System with default settings")
+         _exchange_optimizer = setup_exchange_optimization()
+         
+     return _exchange_optimizer
+ 
+ # Export main classes
+ __all__ = [
+     'ExchangeProfiler',
+     'OrderTypeOptimizer',
+     'SmartOrderRouter',
+     'ExchangeAnalytics',
+     'setup_exchange_optimization',
+     'get_exchange_optimizer'
+ ] 
```

Now, let's create the Exchange Profiler module, which is responsible for analyzing and profiling each exchange's capabilities, latency, and reliability:

---

_****_

```diff
- 
+ """
+ Exchange Profiler
+ ---------------
+ Analyzes and catalogs the capabilities, latency, and reliability of each exchange.
+ """
+ 
+ import time
+ import logging
+ import threading
+ from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Set
+ from datetime import datetime, timedelta
+ from collections import deque
+ 
+ # Configure logging
+ logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
+ 
+ class ExchangeCapabilities:
+     """
+     Represents the capabilities of a specific exchange.
+     """
+     def __init__(self, exchange_id: str):
+         """
+         Initialize exchange capabilities.
+         
+         Args:
+             exchange_id: Exchange identifier
+         """
+         self.exchange_id = exchange_id
+         
+         # Basic information
+         self.name = ""
+         self.description = ""
+         self.url = ""
+         self.api_docs_url = ""
+         
+         # Supported operations
+         self.supports_market_orders = True
+         self.supports_limit_orders = True
+         self.supports_stop_loss = False
+         self.supports_trailing_stop = False
+         self.supports_iceberg_orders = False
+         self.supports_post_only = False
+         self.supports_fill_or_kill = False
+         self.supports_immediate_or_cancel = False
+         self.supports_reduce_only = False
+         
+         # Rate limits
+         self.rate_limits = {
+             "order_creation": {"requests": 10, "period_seconds": 1},
+             "order_cancellation": {"requests": 10, "period_seconds": 1},
+             "market_data": {"requests": 20, "period_seconds": 1}
+         }
+         
+         # Fee structure
+         self.maker_fee = 0.001  # 0.1%
+         self.taker_fee = 0.001  # 0.1%
+         self.fee_tier_requirements = []
+         self.fee_discounts = {}
+         
+         # Order book characteristics
+         self.max_order_book_depth = 100
+         self.order_book_update_frequency_ms = 100
+         
+         # Minimum order sizes
+         self.min_order_sizes = {}  # symbol -> minimum size
+         
+         # Trading pairs
+         self.supported_pairs = set()
+     
+     def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+         """Convert capabilities to dictionary format."""
+         result = vars(self).copy()
+         result["supported_pairs"] = list(self.supported_pairs)
+         return result
+ 
+ class ExchangePerformanceMetrics:
+     """
+     Tracks performance metrics for an exchange.
+     """
+     def __init__(self, max_history: int = 1000):
+         """
+         Initialize performance metrics.
+         
+         Args:
+             max_history: Maximum number of data points to keep
+         """
+         self.max_history = max_history
+         
+         # Latency metrics (in milliseconds)
+         self.order_creation_latency = deque(maxlen=max_history)
+         self.order_cancellation_latency = deque(maxlen=max_history)
+         self.market_data_latency = deque(maxlen=max_history)
+         
+         # Reliability metrics
+         self.order_creation_errors = deque(maxlen=max_history)
+         self.order_cancellation_errors = deque(maxlen=max_history)
+         self.market_data_errors = deque(maxlen=max_history)
+         
+         # Execution quality metrics
+         self.slippage_bps = deque(maxlen=max_history)  # Slippage in basis points
+         self.fill_rates = deque(maxlen=max_history)    # Fill rates for limit orders (0-1)
+         self.rejection_rates = deque(maxlen=max_history)  # Order rejection rates (0-1)
+         
+         # Timestamps for tracking
+         self.timestamps = deque(maxlen=max_history)
+     
+     def add_latency_metric(self, operation: str, latency_ms: float):
+         """Record a latency metric."""
+         now = datetime.utcnow()
+         self.timestamps.append(now)
+         
+         if operation == "order_creation":
+             self.order_creation_latency.append(latency_ms)
+         elif operation == "order_cancellation":
+             self.order_cancellation_latency.append(latency_ms)
+         elif operation == "market_data":
+             self.market_data_latency.append(latency_ms)
+     
+     def add_error(self, operation: str, error_type: str):
+         """Record an error event."""
+         now = datetime.utcnow()
+         error_record = {"timestamp": now, "type": error_type}
+         
+         if operation == "order_creation":
+             self.order_creation_errors.append(error_record)
+         elif operation == "order_cancellation":
+             self.order_cancellation_errors.append(error_record)
+         elif operation == "market_data":
+             self.market_data_errors.append(error_record)
+     
+     def add_execution_metric(self, metric_type: str, value: float):
+         """Record an execution quality metric."""
+         now = datetime.utcnow()
+         self.timestamps.append(now)
+         
+         if metric_type == "slippage_bps":
+             self.slippage_bps.append(value)
+         elif metric_type == "fill_rate":
+             self.fill_rates.append(value)
+         elif metric_type == "rejection_rate":
+             self.rejection_rates.append(value)
+     
+     def get_average_latency(self, operation: str, window_seconds: int = None) -> float:
+         """Get average latency for an operation."""
+         if operation == "order_creation" and self.order_creation_latency:
+             data = self.order_creation_latency
+         elif operation == "order_cancellation" and self.order_cancellation_latency:
+             data = self.order_cancellation_latency
+         elif operation == "market_data" and self.market_data_latency:
+             data = self.market_data_latency
+         else:
+             return 0.0
+         
+         # Apply time window filter if specified
+         if window_seconds and self.timestamps:
+             cutoff = datetime.utcnow() - timedelta(seconds=window_seconds)
+             filtered_data = []
+             for i, ts in enumerate(self.timestamps):
+                 if ts >= cutoff and i < len(data):
+                     filtered_data.append(data[i])
+             data = filtered_data
+         
+         if not data:
+             return 0.0
+         
+         return sum(data) / len(data)
+     
+     def get_error_rate(self, operation: str, window_seconds: int = None) -> float:
+         """Get error rate for an operation (0-1)."""
+         if operation == "order_creation":
+             errors = self.order_creation_errors
+         elif operation == "order_cancellation":
+             errors = self.order_cancellation_errors
+         elif operation == "market_data":
+             errors = self.market_data_errors
+         else:
+             return 0.0
+         
+         # Apply time window filter if specified
+         if window_seconds:
+             cutoff = datetime.utcnow() - timedelta(seconds=window_seconds)
+             errors = [e for e in errors if e["timestamp"] >= cutoff]
+         
+         if not self.timestamps:
+             return 0.0
+         
+         return len(errors) / len(self.timestamps)
+     
+     def get_execution_quality_metric(self, metric_type: str, window_seconds: int = None) -> float:
+         """Get execution quality metric."""
+         if metric_type == "slippage_bps" and self.slippage_bps:
+             data = self.slippage_bps
+         elif metric_type == "fill_rate" and self.fill_rates:
+             data = self.fill_rates
+         elif metric_type == "rejection_rate" and self.rejection_rates:
+             data = self.rejection_rates
+         else:
+             return 0.0
+         
+         # Apply time window filter if specified
+         if window_seconds and self.timestamps:
+             cutoff = datetime.utcnow() - timedelta(seconds=window_seconds)
+             filtered_data = []
+             for i, ts in enumerate(self.timestamps):
+                 if ts >= cutoff and i < len(data):
+                     filtered_data.append(data[i])
+             data = filtered_data
+         
+         if not data:
+             return 0.0
+         
+         return sum(data) / len(data)
+ 
+ class ExchangeProfiler:
+     """
+     Analyzes and catalogs exchange capabilities, latency, and reliability.
+     
+     Key features:
+     1. Exchange capability registration and discovery
+     2. Latency and reliability profiling
+     3. Real-time performance monitoring
+     4. Exchange ranking based on performance metrics
+     """
+     
+     def __init__(
+         self,
+         observability_manager=None,
+         enable_latency_tracking: bool = True,
+         probe_interval_seconds: int = 60
+     ):
+         """
+         Initialize the exchange profiler.
+         
+         Args:
+             observability_manager: Optional observability manager for metrics and tracing
+             enable_latency_tracking: Whether to track latency statistics
+             probe_interval_seconds: How often to probe exchanges for capabilities
+         """
+         self.obs_manager = observability_manager
+         self.enable_latency_tracking = enable_latency_tracking
+         self.probe_interval_seconds = probe_interval_seconds
+         
+         # Exchange registry
+         self.exchanges: Dict[str, ExchangeCapabilities] = {}
+         
+         # Performance metrics
+         self.performance_metrics: Dict[str, ExchangePerformanceMetrics] = {}
+         
+         # Real-time status tracking
+         self.exchange_status: Dict[str, str] = {}  # exchange_id -> "online" | "degraded" | "offline"
+         
+         # Symbol support tracking
+         self.symbol_exchange_map: Dict[str, Set[str]] = {}  # symbol -> set of exchange_ids
+         
+         # Lock for thread safety
+         self._lock = threading.RLock()
+         
+         # Setup metrics
+         self._setup_metrics()
+         
+         # Start background monitoring
+         if enable_latency_tracking:
+             self._start_background_monitoring()
+     
+     def _setup_metrics(self):
+         """Set up observability metrics."""
+         self.metrics = {}
+         
+         if not self.obs_manager:
+             return
+         
+         # Create metrics
+         self.metrics["exchange_latency"] = self.obs_manager.create_histogram(
+             name="exchange_latency_ms",
+             description="Exchange operation latency in milliseconds",
+             labels=["exchange", "operation"]
+         )
+         
+         self.metrics["exchange_errors"] = self.obs_manager.create_counter(
+             name="exchange_errors_total",
+             description="Exchange operation errors",
+             labels=["exchange", "operation", "error_type"]
+         )
+         
+         self.metrics["exchange_fill_rate"] = self.obs_manager.create_gauge(
+             name="exchange_fill_rate",
+             description="Exchange fill rate for limit orders (0-1)",
+             labels=["exchange"]
+         )
+         
+         self.metrics["exchange_rejection_rate"] = self.obs_manager.create_gauge(
+             name="exchange_rejection_rate",
+             description="Exchange order rejection rate (0-1)",
+             labels=["exchange"]
+         )
+         
+         self.metrics["exchange_status"] = self.obs_manager.create_gauge(
+             name="exchange_status",
+             description="Exchange status (0=offline, 1=degraded, 2=online)",
+             labels=["exchange"]
+         )
+     
+     def _start_background_monitoring(self):
+         """Start background thread for exchange monitoring."""
+         # Implement periodic monitoring here if needed
+         pass
+     
+     def register_exchange(self, exchange_id: str, capabilities: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> ExchangeCapabilities:
+         """
+         Register an exchange with the profiler.
+         
+         Args:
+             exchange_id: Unique identifier for the exchange
+             capabilities: Optional pre-defined capabilities
+             
+         Returns:
+             ExchangeCapabilities object for the exchange
+         """
+         with self._lock:
+             if exchange_id in self.exchanges:
+                 logger.info(f"Exchange {exchange_id} already registered, updating capabilities")
+                 exchange_capabilities = self.exchanges[exchange_id]
+             else:
+                 logger.info(f"Registering new exchange: {exchange_id}")
+                 exchange_capabilities = ExchangeCapabilities(exchange_id)
+                 self.exchanges[exchange_id] = exchange_capabilities
+                 self.performance_metrics[exchange_id] = ExchangePerformanceMetrics()
+                 self.exchange_status[exchange_id] = "online"  # Assume online until proven otherwise
+             
+             # Update capabilities if provided
+             if capabilities:
+                 for key, value in capabilities.items():
+                     if hasattr(exchange_capabilities, key):
+                         setattr(exchange_capabilities, key, value)
+             
+             # Update symbol mapping
+             for symbol in exchange_capabilities.supported_pairs:
+                 if symbol not in self.symbol_exchange_map:
+                     self.symbol_exchange_map[symbol] = set()
+                 self.symbol_exchange_map[symbol].add(exchange_id)
+             
+             # Record metrics
+             if self.obs_manager and self.metrics.get("exchange_status"):
+                 status_value = 2 if self.exchange_status[exchange_id] == "online" else \
+                               1 if self.exchange_status[exchange_id] == "degraded" else 0
+                 self.metrics["exchange_status"].labels(exchange=exchange_id).set(status_value)
+             
+             return exchange_capabilities
+     
+     def update_exchange_status(self, exchange_id: str, status: str):
+         """
+         Update the operational status of an exchange.
+         
+         Args:
+             exchange_id: Exchange identifier
+             status: One of "online", "degraded", "offline"
+         """
+         with self._lock:
+             if exchange_id not in self.exchanges:
+                 logger.warning(f"Attempted to update status for unknown exchange: {exchange_id}")
+                 return
+             
+             old_status = self.exchange_status.get(exchange_id, "unknown")
+             self.exchange_status[exchange_id] = status
+             
+             logger.info(f"Exchange {exchange_id} status changed from {old_status} to {status}")
+             
+             # Record metrics
+             if self.obs_manager and self.metrics.get("exchange_status"):
+                 status_value = 2 if status == "online" else 1 if status == "degraded" else 0
+                 self.metrics["exchange_status"].labels(exchange=exchange_id).set(status_value)
+     
+     def record_latency(self, exchange_id: str, operation: str, latency_ms: float):
+         """
+         Record operation latency for an exchange.
+         
+         Args:
+             exchange_id: Exchange identifier
+             operation: Operation type (order_creation, order_cancellation, market_data)
+             latency_ms: Latency in milliseconds
+         """
+         if not self.enable_latency_tracking:
+             return
+         
+         with self._lock:
+             if exchange_id not in self.performance_metrics:
+                 logger.warning(f"Attempted to record latency for unknown exchange: {exchange_id}")
+                 self.performance_metrics[exchange_id] = ExchangePerformanceMetrics()
+             
+             metrics = self.performance_metrics[exchange_id]
+             metrics.add_latency_metric(operation, latency_ms)
+             
+             # Record metrics
+             if self.obs_manager and self.metrics.get("exchange_latency"):
+                 self.metrics["exchange_latency"].labels(
+                     exchange=exchange_id, 
+                     operation=operation
+                 ).observe(latency_ms)
+     
+     def record_error(self, exchange_id: str, operation: str, error_type: str):
+         """
+         Record an operation error for an exchange.
+         
+         Args:
+             exchange_id: Exchange identifier
+             operation: Operation type (order_creation, order_cancellation, market_data)
+             error_type: Type of error encountered
+         """
+         with self._lock:
+             if exchange_id not in self.performance_metrics:
+                 logger.warning(f"Attempted to record error for unknown exchange: {exchange_id}")
+                 self.performance_metrics[exchange_id] = ExchangePerformanceMetrics()
+             
+             metrics = self.performance_metrics[exchange_id]
+             metrics.add_error(operation, error_type)
+             
+             # Record metrics
+             if self.obs_manager and self.metrics.get("exchange_errors"):
+                 self.metrics["exchange_errors"].labels(
+                     exchange=exchange_id, 
+                     operation=operation,
+                     error_type=error_type
+                 ).inc()
+     
+     def record_execution_metric(self, exchange_id: str, metric_type: str, value: float):
+         """
+         Record execution quality metric for an exchange.
+         
+         Args:
+             exchange_id: Exchange identifier
+             metric_type: Type of metric (slippage_bps, fill_rate, rejection_rate)
+             value: Metric value
+         """
+         with self._lock:
+             if exchange_id not in self.performance_metrics:
+                 logger.warning(f"Attempted to record execution metric for unknown exchange: {exchange_id}")
+                 self.performance_metrics[exchange_id] = ExchangePerformanceMetrics()
+             
+             metrics = self.performance_metrics[exchange_id]
+             metrics.add_execution_metric(metric_type, value)
+             
+             # Record metrics
+             if self.obs_manager:
+                 if metric_type == "fill_rate" and self.metrics.get("exchange_fill_rate"):
+                     self.metrics["exchange_fill_rate"].labels(exchange=exchange_id).set(value)
+                 elif metric_type == "rejection_rate" and self.metrics.get("exchange_rejection_rate"):
+                     self.metrics["exchange_rejection_rate"].labels(exchange=exchange_id).set(value)
+     
+     def get_exchange_performance(self, exchange_id: str, window_seconds: int = None) -> Dict[str, float]:
+         """
+         Get performance metrics for an exchange.
+         
+         Args:
+             exchange_id: Exchange identifier
+             window_seconds: Optional time window in seconds
+             
+         Returns:
+             Dict of performance metrics
+         """
+         with self._lock:
+             if exchange_id not in self.performance_metrics:
+                 logger.warning(f"Attempted to get performance for unknown exchange: {exchange_id}")
+                 return {}
+             
+             metrics = self.performance_metrics[exchange_id]
+             
+             return {
+                 "order_creation_latency": metrics.get_average_latency("order_creation", window_seconds),
+                 "order_cancellation_latency": metrics.get_average_latency("order_cancellation", window_seconds),
+                 "market_data_latency": metrics.get_average_latency("market_data", window_seconds),
+                 "order_creation_error_rate": metrics.get_error_rate("order_creation", window_seconds),
+                 "order_cancellation_error_rate": metrics.get_error_rate("order_cancellation", window_seconds),
+                 "market_data_error_rate": metrics.get_error_rate("market_data", window_seconds),
+                 "slippage_bps": metrics.get_execution_quality_metric("slippage_bps", window_seconds),
+                 "fill_rate": metrics.get_execution_quality_metric("fill_rate", window_seconds),
+                 "rejection_rate": metrics.get_execution_quality_metric("rejection_rate", window_seconds)
+             }
+     
+     def get_best_exchange_for_symbol(self, symbol: str, criteria: str = "latency") -> Optional[str]:
+         """
+         Find the best exchange for trading a specific symbol.
+         
+         Args:
+             symbol: Trading symbol
+             criteria: Selection criteria (latency, reliability, cost)
+             
+         Returns:
+             Exchange ID of the best exchange or None if no suitable exchange found
+         """
+         with self._lock:
+             if symbol not in self.symbol_exchange_map:
+                 logger.warning(f"No exchanges support symbol: {symbol}")
+                 return None
+             
+             exchange_candidates = [
+                 ex_id for ex_id in self.symbol_exchange_map[symbol]
+                 if self.exchange_status.get(ex_id) != "offline"
+             ]
+             
+             if not exchange_candidates:
+                 logger.warning(f"All exchanges supporting {symbol} are offline")
+                 return None
+             
+             # Select based on criteria
+             if criteria == "latency":
+                 # Find exchange with lowest order creation latency
+                 lowest_latency = float('inf')
+                 best_exchange = None
+                 
+                 for ex_id in exchange_candidates:
+                     latency = self.performance_metrics[ex_id].get_average_latency("order_creation")
+                     if latency < lowest_latency:
+                         lowest_latency = latency
+                         best_exchange = ex_id
+                 
+                 return best_exchange
+             
+             elif criteria == "reliability":
+                 # Find exchange with lowest error rate
+                 lowest_error_rate = float('inf')
+                 best_exchange = None
+                 
+                 for ex_id in exchange_candidates:
+                     error_rate = self.performance_metrics[ex_id].get_error_rate("order_creation")
+                     if error_rate < lowest_error_rate:
+                         lowest_error_rate = error_rate
+                         best_exchange = ex_id
+                 
+                 return best_exchange
+             
+             elif criteria == "cost":
+                 # Find exchange with lowest trading fees
+                 lowest_fee = float('inf')
+                 best_exchange = None
+                 
+                 for ex_id in exchange_candidates:
+                     taker_fee = self.exchanges[ex_id].taker_fee
+                     if taker_fee < lowest_fee:
+                         lowest_fee = taker_fee
+                         best_exchange = ex_id
+                 
+                 return best_exchange
+             
+             else:
+                 # Default to first available exchange
+                 return exchange_candidates[0]
+     
+     def get_exchanges_for_symbol(self, symbol: str, include_offline: bool = False) -> List[str]:
+         """
+         Get all exchanges supporting a specific symbol.
+         
+         Args:
+             symbol: Trading symbol
+             include_offline: Whether to include offline exchanges
+             
+         Returns:
+             List of exchange IDs
+         """
+         with self._lock:
+             if symbol not in self.symbol_exchange_map:
+                 return []
+             
+             if include_offline:
+                 return list(self.symbol_exchange_map[symbol])
+             else:
+                 return [
+                     ex_id for ex_id in self.symbol_exchange_map[symbol]
+                     if self.exchange_status.get(ex_id) != "offline"
+                 ]
+     
+     def get_exchange_capabilities(self, exchange_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
+         """
+         Get capabilities for a specific exchange.
+         
+         Args:
+             exchange_id: Exchange identifier
+             
+         Returns:
+             Dict of exchange capabilities or None if exchange not found
+         """
+         with self._lock:
+             if exchange_id not in self.exchanges:
+                 logger.warning(f"Requested capabilities for unknown exchange: {exchange_id}")
+                 return None
+             
+             return self.exchanges[exchange_id].to_dict()
+     
+     def get_all_exchanges(self, include_offline: bool = False) -> List[str]:
+         """
+         Get all registered exchanges.
+         
+         Args:
+             include_offline: Whether to include offline exchanges
+             
+         Returns:
+             List of exchange IDs
+         """
+         with self._lock:
+             if include_offline:
+                 return list(self.exchanges.keys())
+             else:
+                 return [
+                     ex_id for ex_id, status in self.exchange_status.items()
+                     if status != "offline"
+                 ] 
```

Now, let's create the Order Type Optimizer module, which will optimize order execution based on exchange-specific features:

```diff
- 
+ """
+ Order Type Optimizer
+ ------------------
+ Selects the optimal order type for a given trading scenario
+ based on exchange features, market conditions, and execution goals.
+ """
+ 
+ import logging
+ import threading
+ from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple, Any
+ from enum import Enum
+ 
+ # Configure logging
+ logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
+ 
+ class OrderExecutionGoal(Enum):
+     """Enumeration of order execution goals."""
+     MINIMIZE_COST = "minimize_cost"
+     MAXIMIZE_FILL_PROBABILITY = "maximize_fill_probability"
+     MINIMIZE_MARKET_IMPACT = "minimize_market_impact"
+     MINIMIZE_TIME_TO_FILL = "minimize_time_to_fill"
+     STEALTH = "stealth"
+     PRICE_IMPROVEMENT = "price_improvement"
+ 
+ class OrderTypeStrategy:
+     """
+     Represents a strategy for using a specific order type.
+     """
+     def __init__(
+         self, 
+         order_type: str,
+         exchange_id: str,
+         description: str,
+         parameters: Dict[str, Any] = None,
+         conditions: Dict[str, Any] = None
+     ):
+         """
+         Initialize order type strategy.
+         
+         Args:
+             order_type: Type of order (e.g., "limit", "market", "iceberg")
+             exchange_id: Exchange identifier
+             description: Human-readable description of the strategy
+             parameters: Parameters for order execution (e.g., time_in_force, post_only)
+             conditions: Market conditions where this strategy is optimal
+         """
+         self.order_type = order_type
+         self.exchange_id = exchange_id
+         self.description = description
+         self.parameters = parameters or {}
+         self.conditions = conditions or {}
+         
+         # Score mapping for different execution goals (0-100)
+         self.goal_scores = {goal: 0 for goal in OrderExecutionGoal}
+     
+     def set_goal_score(self, goal: OrderExecutionGoal, score: int):
+         """Set the score for a specific execution goal."""
+         if not isinstance(goal, OrderExecutionGoal):
+             goal = OrderExecutionGoal(goal)
+         
+         self.goal_scores[goal] = max(0, min(100, score))
+     
+     def get_goal_score(self, goal: OrderExecutionGoal) -> int:
+         """Get the score for a specific execution goal."""
+         if not isinstance(goal, OrderExecutionGoal):
+             goal = OrderExecutionGoal(goal)
+         
+         return self.goal_scores.get(goal, 0)
+     
+     def meets_conditions(self, market_state: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
+         """
+         Check if current market state meets the conditions for this strategy.
+         
+         Args:
+             market_state: Current market conditions
+             
+         Returns:
+             True if conditions are met, False otherwise
+         """
+         for condition_key, condition_value in self.conditions.items():
+             if condition_key not in market_state:
+                 return False
+             
+             market_value = market_state[condition_key]
+             
+             # Handle different condition types
+             if isinstance(condition_value, dict):
+                 if "min" in condition_value and market_value < condition_value["min"]:
+                     return False
+                 if "max" in condition_value and market_value > condition_value["max"]:
+                     return False
+             elif market_value != condition_value:
+                 return False
+         
+         return True
+ 
+ class OrderTypeOptimizer:
+     """
+     Selects the optimal order type based on exchange features, market conditions,
+     and execution goals.
+     
+     Key features:
+     1. Exchange-specific order type selection
+     2. Market condition-aware optimization
+     3. Goal-based strategy selection
+     4. Multi-factor scoring and ranking
+     """
+     
+     def __init__(
+         self,
+         observability_manager=None,
+         exchange_profiler=None
+     ):
+         """
+         Initialize the order type optimizer.
+         
+         Args:
+             observability_manager: Optional observability manager for metrics and tracing
+             exchange_profiler: Optional exchange profiler for capabilities
+         """
+         self.obs_manager = observability_manager
+         self.exchange_profiler = exchange_profiler
+         
+         # Strategy registry
+         self.strategies: Dict[str, List[OrderTypeStrategy]] = {}  # exchange_id -> list of strategies
+         self.strategy_cache: Dict[str, Dict[str, OrderTypeStrategy]] = {}  # (exchange, goal) -> best strategy
+         
+         # Lock for thread safety
+         self._lock = threading.RLock()
+         
+         # Setup metrics
+         self._setup_metrics()
+         
+         # Register default strategies
+         self._register_default_strategies()
+     
+     def _setup_metrics(self):
+         """Set up observability metrics."""
+         self.metrics = {}
+         
+         if not self.obs_manager:
+             return
+         
+         # Create metrics
+         self.metrics["strategy_selection"] = self.obs_manager.create_counter(
+             name="order_type_strategy_selections",
+             description="Count of strategy selections by exchange and goal",
+             labels=["exchange", "goal", "order_type"]
+         )
+         
+         self.metrics["strategy_score"] = self.obs_manager.create_histogram(
+             name="order_type_strategy_score",
+             description="Score of selected strategy",
+             labels=["exchange", "goal", "order_type"]
+         )
+     
+     def _register_default_strategies(self):
+         """Register default order type strategies."""
+         # This would register default strategies for common exchanges
+         # For this example, we'll add a few basic strategies
+         
+         # Example for a generic exchange
+         generic_strategies = [
+             # Simple limit order strategy
+             OrderTypeStrategy(
+                 order_type="limit",
+                 exchange_id="generic",
+                 description="Standard limit order for general use",
+                 parameters={"time_in_force": "GTC"}
+             ),
+             
+             # Market order strategy
+             OrderTypeStrategy(
+                 order_type="market",
+                 exchange_id="generic",
+                 description="Standard market order for immediate execution",
+                 parameters={}
+             ),
+             
+             # Post-only limit order strategy
+             OrderTypeStrategy(
+                 order_type="limit",
+                 exchange_id="generic",
+                 description="Post-only limit order to avoid taker fees",
+                 parameters={"post_only": True, "time_in_force": "GTC"}
+             )
+         ]
+         
+         # Set scores for generic strategies
+         generic_strategies[0].set_goal_score(OrderExecutionGoal.MINIMIZE_COST, 80)
+         generic_strategies[0].set_goal_score(OrderExecutionGoal.MAXIMIZE_FILL_PROBABILITY, 60)
+         generic_strategies[0].set_goal_score(OrderExecutionGoal.MINIMIZE_MARKET_IMPACT, 70)
+         
+         generic_strategies[1].set_goal_score(OrderExecutionGoal.MINIMIZE_TIME_TO_FILL, 95)
+         generic_strategies[1].set_goal_score(OrderExecutionGoal.MAXIMIZE_FILL_PROBABILITY, 90)
+         
+         generic_strategies[2].set_goal_score(OrderExecutionGoal.MINIMIZE_COST, 90)
+         generic_strategies[2].set_goal_score(OrderExecutionGoal.MINIMIZE_MARKET_IMPACT, 75)
+         
+         # Register these strategies
+         with self._lock:
+             self.strategies["generic"] = generic_strategies
+     
+     def register_strategy(self, strategy: OrderTypeStrategy):
+         """
+         Register a new order type strategy.
+         
+         Args:
+             strategy: The strategy to register
+         """
+         with self._lock:
+             exchange_id = strategy.exchange_id
+             
+             if exchange_id not in self.strategies:
+                 self.strategies[exchange_id] = []
+             
+             # Check if strategy already exists
+             for i, existing in enumerate(self.strategies[exchange_id]):
+                 if (existing.order_type == strategy.order_type and 
+                     existing.parameters == strategy.parameters):
+                     # Replace existing strategy
+                     self.strategies[exchange_id][i] = strategy
+                     logger.info(f"Updated existing strategy for {exchange_id}: {strategy.description}")
+                     
+                     # Clear cache
+                     self.strategy_cache = {}
+                     return
+             
+             # Add new strategy
+             self.strategies[exchange_id].append(strategy)
+             logger.info(f"Registered new strategy for {exchange_id}: {strategy.description}")
+             
+             # Clear cache
+             self.strategy_cache = {}
+     
+     def get_best_order_type(
+         self,
+         exchange_id: str,
+         symbol: str,
+         goal: OrderExecutionGoal,
+         side: str,
+         size: float,
+         market_state: Dict[str, Any] = None
+     ) -> Optional[Tuple[str, Dict[str, Any]]]:
+         """
+         Get the best order type for the given parameters.
+         
+         Args:
+             exchange_id: Exchange identifier
+             symbol: Trading symbol
+             goal: Order execution goal
+             side: Order side ("buy" or "sell")
+             size: Order size
+             market_state: Current market conditions
+             
+         Returns:
+             Tuple of (order_type, parameters) or None if no suitable strategy found
+         """
+         with self._lock:
+             # Normalize exchange_id
+             if exchange_id not in self.strategies:
+                 # Fall back to generic if specific exchange not found
+                 exchange_id = "generic"
+                 
+                 if exchange_id not in self.strategies:
+                     logger.warning(f"No strategies available for {exchange_id} or generic")
+                     return None
+             
+             # Normalize goal
+             if not isinstance(goal, OrderExecutionGoal):
+                 try:
+                     goal = OrderExecutionGoal(goal)
+                 except ValueError:
+                     logger.warning(f"Invalid goal: {goal}")
+                     return None
+             
+             # Use market state or empty dict
+             market_state = market_state or {}
+             
+             # Add side and size to market state for condition checking
+             market_state["side"] = side
+             market_state["size"] = size
+             market_state["symbol"] = symbol
+             
+             # Check cache first
+             cache_key = f"{exchange_id}:{goal.value}:{symbol}:{side}"
+             
+             if cache_key in self.strategy_cache:
+                 strategy = self.strategy_cache[cache_key]
+                 
+                 # Verify conditions still met
+                 if strategy.meets_conditions(market_state):
+                     logger.debug(f"Using cached strategy for {exchange_id}: {strategy.description}")
+                     
+                     # Track metric
+                     if self.obs_manager and self.metrics.get("strategy_selection"):
+                         self.metrics["strategy_selection"].labels(
+                             exchange=exchange_id,
+                             goal=goal.value,
+                             order_type=strategy.order_type
+                         ).inc()
+                     
+                     return strategy.order_type, strategy.parameters.copy()
+             
+             # Find strategies that meet conditions
+             viable_strategies = []
+             
+             for strategy in self.strategies[exchange_id]:
+                 if strategy.meets_conditions(market_state):
+                     viable_strategies.append(strategy)
+             
+             if not viable_strategies:
+                 logger.warning(f"No viable strategies for {exchange_id} with goal {goal.value}")
+                 return None
+             
+             # Rank strategies by goal score
+             best_strategy = max(viable_strategies, key=lambda s: s.get_goal_score(goal))
+             score = best_strategy.get_goal_score(goal)
+             
+             logger.info(
+                 f"Selected best strategy for {exchange_id} with goal {goal.value}: "
+                 f"{best_strategy.description} (score: {score})"
+             )
+             
+             # Cache result
+             self.strategy_cache[cache_key] = best_strategy
+             
+             # Track metrics
+             if self.obs_manager:
+                 if self.metrics.get("strategy_selection"):
+                     self.metrics["strategy_selection"].labels(
+                         exchange=exchange_id,
+                         goal=goal.value,
+                         order_type=best_strategy.order_type
+                     ).inc()
+                 
+                 if self.metrics.get("strategy_score"):
+                     self.metrics["strategy_score"].labels(
+                         exchange=exchange_id,
+                         goal=goal.value,
+                         order_type=best_strategy.order_type
+                     ).observe(score)
+             
+             return best_strategy.order_type, best_strategy.parameters.copy()
+     
+     def optimize_order_parameters(
+         self,
+         exchange_id: str,
+         order_type: str,
+         parameters: Dict[str, Any],
+         market_state: Dict[str, Any]
+     ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+         """
+         Further optimize order parameters based on market conditions.
+         
+         Args:
+             exchange_id: Exchange identifier
+             order_type: Type of order
+             parameters: Initial order parameters
+             market_state: Current market conditions
+             
+         Returns:
+             Optimized parameters
+         """
+         # Start with a copy of the original parameters
+         optimized = parameters.copy()
+         
+         # Get exchange capabilities if available
+         exchange_caps = None
+         if self.exchange_profiler:
+             exchange_caps = self.exchange_profiler.get_exchange_capabilities(exchange_id)
+         
+         # Optimize based on order type and market state
+         if order_type == "limit":
+             # Example: Adjust time_in_force based on volatility
+             if "volatility" in market_state and market_state["volatility"] > 0.5:
+                 optimized["time_in_force"] = "IOC"  # Immediate-or-Cancel in volatile markets
+             
+             # Example: Adjust post_only based on spread
+             if "spread_bps" in market_state:
+                 if market_state["spread_bps"] < 5:  # Tight spread
+                     optimized["post_only"] = True
+         
+         elif order_type == "iceberg":
+             # Example: Adjust visible size based on market depth
+             if "market_depth" in market_state:
+                 depth = market_state["market_depth"]
+                 total_size = market_state.get("size", 1.0)
+                 
+                 # Make visible portion proportional to market depth
+                 visible_pct = min(0.3, depth / 100)  # Cap at 30%
+                 optimized["visible_size"] = max(total_size * visible_pct, 0.001)
+         
+         return optimized
+     
+     def get_strategy_description(
+         self,
+         exchange_id: str,
+         order_type: str,
+         parameters: Dict[str, Any]
+     ) -> Optional[str]:
+         """
+         Get the description of a strategy.
+         
+         Args:
+             exchange_id: Exchange identifier
+             order_type: Type of order
+             parameters: Order parameters
+             
+         Returns:
+             Strategy description or None if not found
+         """
+         with self._lock:
+             if exchange_id not in self.strategies:
+                 return None
+             
+             for strategy in self.strategies[exchange_id]:
+                 if (strategy.order_type == order_type and 
+                     all(strategy.parameters.get(k) == v for k, v in parameters.items())):
+                     return strategy.description
+             
+             return None
+     
+     def list_available_strategies(self, exchange_id: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
+         """
+         List all available strategies for an exchange.
+         
+         Args:
+             exchange_id: Exchange identifier
+             
+         Returns:
+             List of strategy details
+         """
+         with self._lock:
+             if exchange_id not in self.strategies:
+                 return []
+             
+             result = []
+             for strategy in self.strategies[exchange_id]:
+                 result.append({
+                     "order_type": strategy.order_type,
+                     "description": strategy.description,
+                     "parameters": strategy.parameters,
+                     "goal_scores": {goal.value: score for goal, score in strategy.goal_scores.items()}
+                 })
+             
+             return result 
```
