```diff
+ """
+ Smart Order Router
+ ----------------
+ Intelligently routes orders to optimal exchanges based on execution criteria,
+ symbol availability, exchange performance, and fee structures.
+ """
+ 
+ import logging
+ import threading
+ from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Tuple, Set
+ from enum import Enum
+ import time
+ from datetime import datetime, timedelta
+ 
+ # Configure logging
+ logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
+ 
+ class RoutingCriteria(Enum):
+     """Enumeration of routing selection criteria."""
+     LOWEST_FEES = "lowest_fees"
+     FASTEST_EXECUTION = "fastest_execution"
+     HIGHEST_RELIABILITY = "highest_reliability"
+     BEST_PRICE = "best_price"
+     BEST_LIQUIDITY = "best_liquidity"
+     SMART = "smart"  # Multi-factor optimization
+ 
+ class RoutingDecision:
+     """
+     Represents a routing decision made by the router.
+     """
+     def __init__(
+         self,
+         exchange_id: str,
+         symbol: str,
+         decision_time: datetime,
+         primary_criterion: RoutingCriteria,
+         score: float,
+         alternatives: Dict[str, float] = None
+     ):
+         """
+         Initialize a routing decision.
+         
+         Args:
+             exchange_id: Selected exchange identifier
+             symbol: Trading symbol
+             decision_time: When the decision was made
+             primary_criterion: Main criterion used for selection
+             score: Score of the selected exchange (0-100)
+             alternatives: Alternative exchanges and their scores
+         """
+         self.exchange_id = exchange_id
+         self.symbol = symbol
+         self.decision_time = decision_time
+         self.primary_criterion = primary_criterion
+         self.score = score
+         self.alternatives = alternatives or {}
+         
+         # Track execution outcome
+         self.execution_outcome = None
+         self.execution_latency_ms = None
+     
+     def set_execution_outcome(self, outcome: str, latency_ms: float = None):
+         """Record the execution outcome for this routing decision."""
+         self.execution_outcome = outcome
+         self.execution_latency_ms = latency_ms
+     
+     def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+         """Convert to dictionary for logging/analysis."""
+         return {
+             "exchange": self.exchange_id,
+             "symbol": self.symbol,
+             "time": self.decision_time.isoformat(),
+             "criterion": self.primary_criterion.value,
+             "score": self.score,
+             "alternatives": self.alternatives,
+             "outcome": self.execution_outcome,
+             "latency_ms": self.execution_latency_ms
+         }
+ 
+ class ExchangePriceData:
+     """
+     Holds price and liquidity data for a specific exchange and symbol.
+     """
+     def __init__(self, exchange_id: str, symbol: str):
+         """
+         Initialize exchange price data.
+         
+         Args:
+             exchange_id: Exchange identifier
+             symbol: Trading symbol
+         """
+         self.exchange_id = exchange_id
+         self.symbol = symbol
+         self.timestamp = datetime.utcnow()
+         
+         # Price data
+         self.bid = 0.0
+         self.ask = 0.0
+         self.last = 0.0
+         
+         # Liquidity data
+         self.bid_liquidity = 0.0  # Total volume available at bid
+         self.ask_liquidity = 0.0  # Total volume available at ask
+         self.last_update = None
+     
+     def update(
+         self,
+         bid: float = None,
+         ask: float = None,
+         last: float = None,
+         bid_liquidity: float = None,
+         ask_liquidity: float = None
+     ):
+         """Update the price data."""
+         self.timestamp = datetime.utcnow()
+         
+         if bid is not None:
+             self.bid = bid
+         
+         if ask is not None:
+             self.ask = ask
+         
+         if last is not None:
+             self.last = last
+         
+         if bid_liquidity is not None:
+             self.bid_liquidity = bid_liquidity
+         
+         if ask_liquidity is not None:
+             self.ask_liquidity = ask_liquidity
+         
+         self.last_update = self.timestamp
+     
+     def is_fresh(self, max_age_seconds: int = 5) -> bool:
+         """Check if the data is fresh enough to use."""
+         if not self.last_update:
+             return False
+         
+         age = datetime.utcnow() - self.last_update
+         return age.total_seconds() <= max_age_seconds
+     
+     def get_mid_price(self) -> float:
+         """Get the mid price."""
+         if self.bid <= 0 or self.ask <= 0:
+             return self.last if self.last > 0 else 0
+         
+         return (self.bid + self.ask) / 2
+ 
+ class SmartOrderRouter:
+     """
+     Routes orders to optimal exchanges based on various criteria.
+     
+     Key features:
+     1. Multi-exchange order routing
+     2. Price-aware selection
+     3. Execution quality optimization
+     4. Cost-based routing
+     5. Performance and reliability tracking
+     """
+     
+     def __init__(
+         self,
+         observability_manager=None,
+         exchange_profiler=None,
+         price_feed=None,
+         default_exchanges: List[str] = None,
+         config: Dict[str, Any] = None
+     ):
+         """
+         Initialize the smart order router.
+         
+         Args:
+             observability_manager: Optional observability manager for metrics and tracing
+             exchange_profiler: Optional exchange profiler for capabilities
+             price_feed: Optional price feed for real-time pricing
+             default_exchanges: List of default exchanges to consider
+             config: Additional configuration parameters
+         """
+         self.obs_manager = observability_manager
+         self.exchange_profiler = exchange_profiler
+         self.price_feed = price_feed
+         self.default_exchanges = default_exchanges or []
+         self.config = config or {}
+         
+         # Exchange registry
+         self.available_exchanges: Set[str] = set(self.default_exchanges)
+         
+         # Price cache
+         self.price_cache: Dict[str, Dict[str, ExchangePriceData]] = {}  # symbol -> exchange -> data
+         
+         # Decision history
+         self.routing_history: List[RoutingDecision] = []
+         self.max_history_size = self.config.get("max_history_size", 1000)
+         
+         # Exchange performance statistics
+         self.exchange_performance: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
+         
+         # Exchange-symbol mapping
+         self.symbol_exchanges: Dict[str, Set[str]] = {}  # symbol -> set of exchanges
+         
+         # Lock for thread safety
+         self._lock = threading.RLock()
+         
+         # Setup metrics
+         self._setup_metrics()
+     
+     def _setup_metrics(self):
+         """Set up observability metrics."""
+         self.metrics = {}
+         
+         if not self.obs_manager:
+             return
+         
+         # Create metrics
+         self.metrics["routing_decisions"] = self.obs_manager.create_counter(
+             name="order_routing_decisions",
+             description="Count of routing decisions by exchange and criterion",
+             labels=["exchange", "criterion", "symbol"]
+         )
+         
+         self.metrics["routing_score"] = self.obs_manager.create_histogram(
+             name="order_routing_score",
+             description="Score of the selected exchange for routing",
+             labels=["exchange", "criterion"]
+         )
+         
+         self.metrics["execution_latency"] = self.obs_manager.create_histogram(
+             name="order_execution_latency_ms",
+             description="Execution latency in milliseconds",
+             labels=["exchange"]
+         )
+         
+         self.metrics["price_advantage"] = self.obs_manager.create_histogram(
+             name="order_price_advantage_bps",
+             description="Price advantage in basis points",
+             labels=["exchange", "side"]
+         )
+     
+     def register_exchange(self, exchange_id: str):
+         """
+         Register an exchange with the router.
+         
+         Args:
+             exchange_id: Exchange identifier
+         """
+         with self._lock:
+             if exchange_id in self.available_exchanges:
+                 logger.debug(f"Exchange {exchange_id} already registered")
+                 return
+             
+             logger.info(f"Registering exchange with router: {exchange_id}")
+             self.available_exchanges.add(exchange_id)
+             self.exchange_performance[exchange_id] = {
+                 "success_rate": 1.0,
+                 "avg_latency_ms": 0,
+                 "price_advantage_bps": 0,
+                 "num_executions": 0
+             }
+     
+     def update_exchange_symbols(self, exchange_id: str, symbols: List[str]):
+         """
+         Update the list of symbols supported by an exchange.
+         
+         Args:
+             exchange_id: Exchange identifier
+             symbols: List of supported symbols
+         """
+         with self._lock:
+             # Make sure exchange is registered
+             if exchange_id not in self.available_exchanges:
+                 self.register_exchange(exchange_id)
+             
+             # Update symbol mapping
+             for symbol in symbols:
+                 if symbol not in self.symbol_exchanges:
+                     self.symbol_exchanges[symbol] = set()
+                 
+                 self.symbol_exchanges[symbol].add(exchange_id)
+             
+             logger.info(f"Updated symbol list for {exchange_id}: {len(symbols)} symbols")
+     
+     def update_price_data(
+         self,
+         exchange_id: str,
+         symbol: str,
+         bid: float = None,
+         ask: float = None,
+         last: float = None,
+         bid_liquidity: float = None,
+         ask_liquidity: float = None
+     ):
+         """
+         Update price and liquidity data for an exchange and symbol.
+         
+         Args:
+             exchange_id: Exchange identifier
+             symbol: Trading symbol
+             bid: Bid price
+             ask: Ask price
+             last: Last trade price
+             bid_liquidity: Total volume available at bid
+             ask_liquidity: Total volume available at ask
+         """
+         with self._lock:
+             # Initialize price cache for symbol if needed
+             if symbol not in self.price_cache:
+                 self.price_cache[symbol] = {}
+             
+             # Initialize exchange data if needed
+             if exchange_id not in self.price_cache[symbol]:
+                 self.price_cache[symbol][exchange_id] = ExchangePriceData(exchange_id, symbol)
+             
+             # Update data
+             price_data = self.price_cache[symbol][exchange_id]
+             price_data.update(
+                 bid=bid,
+                 ask=ask,
+                 last=last,
+                 bid_liquidity=bid_liquidity,
+                 ask_liquidity=ask_liquidity
+             )
+     
+     def update_performance_data(
+         self,
+         exchange_id: str,
+         success_rate: float = None,
+         avg_latency_ms: float = None,
+         price_advantage_bps: float = None
+     ):
+         """
+         Update performance statistics for an exchange.
+         
+         Args:
+             exchange_id: Exchange identifier
+             success_rate: Success rate (0-1)
+             avg_latency_ms: Average execution latency in milliseconds
+             price_advantage_bps: Average price advantage in basis points
+         """
+         with self._lock:
+             # Initialize exchange if needed
+             if exchange_id not in self.exchange_performance:
+                 self.register_exchange(exchange_id)
+             
+             # Update statistics
+             if success_rate is not None:
+                 self.exchange_performance[exchange_id]["success_rate"] = max(0.0, min(1.0, success_rate))
+             
+             if avg_latency_ms is not None:
+                 self.exchange_performance[exchange_id]["avg_latency_ms"] = max(0.0, avg_latency_ms)
+             
+             if price_advantage_bps is not None:
+                 self.exchange_performance[exchange_id]["price_advantage_bps"] = price_advantage_bps
+     
+     def record_execution(
+         self,
+         exchange_id: str,
+         success: bool,
+         latency_ms: float,
+         price_advantage_bps: float = 0.0
+     ):
+         """
+         Record an execution outcome to update performance statistics.
+         
+         Args:
+             exchange_id: Exchange identifier
+             success: Whether the execution was successful
+             latency_ms: Execution latency in milliseconds
+             price_advantage_bps: Price advantage in basis points (can be negative)
+         """
+         with self._lock:
+             # Initialize exchange if needed
+             if exchange_id not in self.exchange_performance:
+                 self.register_exchange(exchange_id)
+             
+             # Get current performance data
+             perf = self.exchange_performance[exchange_id]
+             num_executions = perf["num_executions"]
+             
+             # Calculate new performance values
+             if num_executions == 0:
+                 # First execution
+                 perf["success_rate"] = 1.0 if success else 0.0
+                 perf["avg_latency_ms"] = latency_ms
+                 perf["price_advantage_bps"] = price_advantage_bps
+             else:
+                 # Update with exponential moving average
+                 weight = min(0.1, 1.0 / (num_executions + 1))
+                 
+                 perf["success_rate"] = (1 - weight) * perf["success_rate"] + weight * (1.0 if success else 0.0)
+                 perf["avg_latency_ms"] = (1 - weight) * perf["avg_latency_ms"] + weight * latency_ms
+                 perf["price_advantage_bps"] = (1 - weight) * perf["price_advantage_bps"] + weight * price_advantage_bps
+             
+             # Increment execution count
+             perf["num_executions"] = num_executions + 1
+             
+             # Record metrics
+             if self.obs_manager:
+                 if self.metrics.get("execution_latency"):
+                     self.metrics["execution_latency"].labels(exchange=exchange_id).observe(latency_ms)
+                 
+                 if self.metrics.get("price_advantage"):
+                     side = "buy" if price_advantage_bps > 0 else "sell"
+                     self.metrics["price_advantage"].labels(
+                         exchange=exchange_id, 
+                         side=side
+                     ).observe(abs(price_advantage_bps))
+     
+     def get_exchanges_for_symbol(self, symbol: str) -> List[str]:
+         """
+         Get all exchanges supporting a specific symbol.
+         
+         Args:
+             symbol: Trading symbol
+             
+         Returns:
+             List of exchange IDs
+         """
+         with self._lock:
+             # First check our internal mapping
+             if symbol in self.symbol_exchanges:
+                 return list(self.symbol_exchanges[symbol])
+             
+             # If exchange profiler is available, try that
+             if self.exchange_profiler:
+                 return self.exchange_profiler.get_exchanges_for_symbol(symbol)
+             
+             # Default to all available exchanges
+             return list(self.available_exchanges)
+     
+     def get_best_price(
+         self,
+         symbol: str,
+         side: str,
+         exchanges: List[str] = None
+     ) -> Tuple[Optional[str], float, float]:
+         """
+         Find the exchange with the best price for a symbol and side.
+         
+         Args:
+             symbol: Trading symbol
+             side: Order side ("buy" or "sell")
+             exchanges: Optional list of exchanges to consider
+             
+         Returns:
+             Tuple of (exchange_id, price, liquidity) or (None, 0, 0) if not found
+         """
+         with self._lock:
+             if not exchanges:
+                 exchanges = self.get_exchanges_for_symbol(symbol)
+             
+             best_exchange = None
+             best_price = 0.0
+             best_liquidity = 0.0
+             
+             # Get fresh price data
+             for exchange_id in exchanges:
+                 # Check if we have price data for this exchange
+                 if (symbol in self.price_cache and 
+                     exchange_id in self.price_cache[symbol] and
+                     self.price_cache[symbol][exchange_id].is_fresh()):
+                     
+                     price_data = self.price_cache[symbol][exchange_id]
+                     
+                     if side == "buy":
+                         # When buying, we want the lowest ask price
+                         price = price_data.ask
+                         liquidity = price_data.ask_liquidity
+                         
+                         if price > 0 and (best_price == 0 or price < best_price):
+                             best_exchange = exchange_id
+                             best_price = price
+                             best_liquidity = liquidity
+                     else:
+                         # When selling, we want the highest bid price
+                         price = price_data.bid
+                         liquidity = price_data.bid_liquidity
+                         
+                         if price > 0 and price > best_price:
+                             best_exchange = exchange_id
+                             best_price = price
+                             best_liquidity = liquidity
+             
+             return best_exchange, best_price, best_liquidity
+     
+     def route_order(
+         self,
+         symbol: str,
+         side: str,
+         size: float,
+         routing_criteria: RoutingCriteria = RoutingCriteria.SMART,
+         exchanges: List[str] = None,
+         require_price_data: bool = True
+     ) -> Optional[RoutingDecision]:
+         """
+         Determine the optimal exchange for routing an order.
+         
+         Args:
+             symbol: Trading symbol
+             side: Order side ("buy" or "sell")
+             size: Order size
+             routing_criteria: Criteria for exchange selection
+             exchanges: Optional list of exchanges to consider
+             require_price_data: Whether to require fresh price data
+             
+         Returns:
+             RoutingDecision or None if no suitable exchange found
+         """
+         with self._lock:
+             # Get candidate exchanges
+             if not exchanges:
+                 exchanges = self.get_exchanges_for_symbol(symbol)
+             
+             if not exchanges:
+                 logger.warning(f"No exchanges available for symbol: {symbol}")
+                 return None
+             
+             # If there's only one exchange, return it
+             if len(exchanges) == 1:
+                 exchange_id = exchanges[0]
+                 
+                 decision = RoutingDecision(
+                     exchange_id=exchange_id,
+                     symbol=symbol,
+                     decision_time=datetime.utcnow(),
+                     primary_criterion=routing_criteria,
+                     score=100.0
+                 )
+                 
+                 # Record the decision
+                 self._record_routing_decision(decision)
+                 return decision
+             
+             # Score each exchange based on the criteria
+             exchange_scores = {}
+             
+             # Special case for BEST_PRICE which requires price data
+             if routing_criteria == RoutingCriteria.BEST_PRICE:
+                 best_exchange, best_price, best_liquidity = self.get_best_price(symbol, side, exchanges)
+                 
+                 if not best_exchange and require_price_data:
+                     logger.warning(f"No price data available for {symbol} on any exchange")
+                     return None
+                 
+                 if best_exchange:
+                     # Set highest score for the best price exchange
+                     exchange_scores[best_exchange] = 100.0
+                     
+                     # Score others relative to the best price
+                     for exchange_id in exchanges:
+                         if exchange_id != best_exchange:
+                             # Check if we have price data
+                             if (symbol in self.price_cache and 
+                                 exchange_id in self.price_cache[symbol] and
+                                 self.price_cache[symbol][exchange_id].is_fresh()):
+                                 
+                                 price_data = self.price_cache[symbol][exchange_id]
+                                 exchange_price = price_data.ask if side == "buy" else price_data.bid
+                                 
+                                 if exchange_price > 0:
+                                     # Calculate price difference in basis points
+                                     if side == "buy":
+                                         # Lower is better for buys
+                                         price_diff_bps = (exchange_price - best_price) / best_price * 10000
+                                         score = max(0, 100 - price_diff_bps)
+                                     else:
+                                         # Higher is better for sells
+                                         price_diff_bps = (exchange_price - best_price) / best_price * 10000
+                                         score = max(0, 100 - abs(price_diff_bps))
+                                     
+                                     exchange_scores[exchange_id] = score
+             
+             # Handle BEST_LIQUIDITY criteria
+             elif routing_criteria == RoutingCriteria.BEST_LIQUIDITY:
+                 # Find exchange with best liquidity for the side
+                 best_liquidity = 0.0
+                 best_exchange = None
+                 
+                 for exchange_id in exchanges:
+                     # Check if we have price data
+                     if (symbol in self.price_cache and 
+                         exchange_id in self.price_cache[symbol] and
+                         self.price_cache[symbol][exchange_id].is_fresh()):
+                         
+                         price_data = self.price_cache[symbol][exchange_id]
+                         liquidity = price_data.ask_liquidity if side == "buy" else price_data.bid_liquidity
+                         
+                         if liquidity > best_liquidity:
+                             best_liquidity = liquidity
+                             best_exchange = exchange_id
+                 
+                 if best_exchange and best_liquidity > 0:
+                     # Score based on liquidity available
+                     for exchange_id in exchanges:
+                         if exchange_id == best_exchange:
+                             exchange_scores[exchange_id] = 100.0
+                         elif (symbol in self.price_cache and 
+                               exchange_id in self.price_cache[symbol] and
+                               self.price_cache[symbol][exchange_id].is_fresh()):
+                             
+                             price_data = self.price_cache[symbol][exchange_id]
+                             liquidity = price_data.ask_liquidity if side == "buy" else price_data.bid_liquidity
+                             
+                             # Score as percentage of best liquidity
+                             if best_liquidity > 0:
+                                 exchange_scores[exchange_id] = (liquidity / best_liquidity) * 100
+                             else:
+                                 exchange_scores[exchange_id] = 0.0
+             
+             # Handle LOWEST_FEES criteria
+             elif routing_criteria == RoutingCriteria.LOWEST_FEES:
+                 # Get fee data from exchange profiler if available
+                 if self.exchange_profiler:
+                     lowest_fee = float('inf')
+                     lowest_fee_exchange = None
+                     
+                     for exchange_id in exchanges:
+                         caps = self.exchange_profiler.get_exchange_capabilities(exchange_id)
+                         
+                         if caps:
+                             # Use taker fee for market orders, maker fee for limit orders
+                             fee = caps.get("taker_fee", 0.001)  # Default to 0.1%
+                             
+                             if fee < lowest_fee:
+                                 lowest_fee = fee
+                                 lowest_fee_exchange = exchange_id
+                     
+                     if lowest_fee_exchange:
+                         # Score based on fee comparison
+                         for exchange_id in exchanges:
+                             caps = self.exchange_profiler.get_exchange_capabilities(exchange_id)
+                             
+                             if caps:
+                                 fee = caps.get("taker_fee", 0.001)
+                                 
+                                 # Score inversely proportional to fee (lower fee = higher score)
+                                 if fee > 0:
+                                     exchange_scores[exchange_id] = min(100, (lowest_fee / fee) * 100)
+                                 else:
+                                     exchange_scores[exchange_id] = 100
+                             else:
+                                 exchange_scores[exchange_id] = 50  # Default score for unknown fee
+                 else:
+                     # Use performance statistics if available
+                     for exchange_id in exchanges:
+                         if exchange_id in self.exchange_performance:
+                             # Use price advantage as a proxy for fee effectiveness
+                             advantage = self.exchange_performance[exchange_id].get("price_advantage_bps", 0)
+                             exchange_scores[exchange_id] = min(100, max(0, 50 + advantage / 2))
+                         else:
+                             exchange_scores[exchange_id] = 50  # Default score
+             
+             # Handle FASTEST_EXECUTION criteria
+             elif routing_criteria == RoutingCriteria.FASTEST_EXECUTION:
+                 # Find exchange with lowest latency
+                 lowest_latency = float('inf')
+                 fastest_exchange = None
+                 
+                 for exchange_id in exchanges:
+                     if exchange_id in self.exchange_performance:
+                         latency = self.exchange_performance[exchange_id].get("avg_latency_ms", float('inf'))
+                         
+                         if latency < lowest_latency and latency > 0:
+                             lowest_latency = latency
+                             fastest_exchange = exchange_id
+                 
+                 if fastest_exchange:
+                     # Score based on latency comparison
+                     for exchange_id in exchanges:
+                         if exchange_id in self.exchange_performance:
+                             latency = self.exchange_performance[exchange_id].get("avg_latency_ms", float('inf'))
+                             
+                             if latency > 0:
+                                 # Score inversely proportional to latency (lower latency = higher score)
+                                 # Cap at 100 and ensure minimum score of 10
+                                 exchange_scores[exchange_id] = min(100, max(10, (lowest_latency / latency) * 100))
+                             else:
+                                 exchange_scores[exchange_id] = 50  # Default for unknown latency
+                         else:
+                             exchange_scores[exchange_id] = 50  # Default score
+             
+             # Handle HIGHEST_RELIABILITY criteria
+             elif routing_criteria == RoutingCriteria.HIGHEST_RELIABILITY:
+                 # Find exchange with highest success rate
+                 highest_success = 0.0
+                 most_reliable = None
+                 
+                 for exchange_id in exchanges:
+                     if exchange_id in self.exchange_performance:
+                         success_rate = self.exchange_performance[exchange_id].get("success_rate", 0.0)
+                         
+                         if success_rate > highest_success:
+                             highest_success = success_rate
+                             most_reliable = exchange_id
+                 
+                 if most_reliable:
+                     # Score based on success rate
+                     for exchange_id in exchanges:
+                         if exchange_id in self.exchange_performance:
+                             success_rate = self.exchange_performance[exchange_id].get("success_rate", 0.0)
+                             
+                             # Score directly proportional to success rate
+                             exchange_scores[exchange_id] = success_rate * 100
+                         else:
+                             exchange_scores[exchange_id] = 50  # Default score
+             
+             # Handle SMART criteria (multi-factor)
+             elif routing_criteria == RoutingCriteria.SMART:
+                 # Calculate scores for each factor
+                 price_scores = {}
+                 fee_scores = {}
+                 latency_scores = {}
+                 reliability_scores = {}
+                 
+                 # Get price scores
+                 best_exchange, _, _ = self.get_best_price(symbol, side, exchanges)
+                 
+                 if best_exchange:
+                     price_scores[best_exchange] = 100.0
+                     
+                     # Score others relative to the best price
+                     for exchange_id in exchanges:
+                         if exchange_id != best_exchange:
+                             if (symbol in self.price_cache and 
+                                 exchange_id in self.price_cache[symbol] and
+                                 self.price_cache[symbol][exchange_id].is_fresh()):
+                                 
+                                 price_data = self.price_cache[symbol][exchange_id]
+                                 best_data = self.price_cache[symbol][best_exchange]
+                                 
+                                 if side == "buy":
+                                     # Lower is better for buys
+                                     best_price = best_data.ask
+                                     exchange_price = price_data.ask
+                                     
+                                     if best_price > 0 and exchange_price > 0:
+                                         price_diff_bps = (exchange_price - best_price) / best_price * 10000
+                                         price_scores[exchange_id] = max(0, 100 - price_diff_bps)
+                                 else:
+                                     # Higher is better for sells
+                                     best_price = best_data.bid
+                                     exchange_price = price_data.bid
+                                     
+                                     if best_price > 0 and exchange_price > 0:
+                                         price_diff_bps = (best_price - exchange_price) / best_price * 10000
+                                         price_scores[exchange_id] = max(0, 100 - price_diff_bps)
+                 
+                 # Get fee scores
+                 if self.exchange_profiler:
+                     lowest_fee = float('inf')
+                     
+                     for exchange_id in exchanges:
+                         caps = self.exchange_profiler.get_exchange_capabilities(exchange_id)
+                         
+                         if caps:
+                             fee = caps.get("taker_fee", 0.001)
+                             
+                             if fee < lowest_fee:
+                                 lowest_fee = fee
+                     
+                     if lowest_fee < float('inf'):
+                         for exchange_id in exchanges:
+                             caps = self.exchange_profiler.get_exchange_capabilities(exchange_id)
+                             
+                             if caps:
+                                 fee = caps.get("taker_fee", 0.001)
+                                 
+                                 if fee > 0:
+                                     fee_scores[exchange_id] = min(100, (lowest_fee / fee) * 100)
+                 
+                 # Get latency scores
+                 lowest_latency = float('inf')
+                 
+                 for exchange_id in exchanges:
+                     if exchange_id in self.exchange_performance:
+                         latency = self.exchange_performance[exchange_id].get("avg_latency_ms", float('inf'))
+                         
+                         if latency < lowest_latency and latency > 0:
+                             lowest_latency = latency
+                 
+                 if lowest_latency < float('inf'):
+                     for exchange_id in exchanges:
+                         if exchange_id in self.exchange_performance:
+                             latency = self.exchange_performance[exchange_id].get("avg_latency_ms", float('inf'))
+                             
+                             if latency > 0:
+                                 latency_scores[exchange_id] = min(100, max(10, (lowest_latency / latency) * 100))
+                 
+                 # Get reliability scores
+                 for exchange_id in exchanges:
+                     if exchange_id in self.exchange_performance:
+                         success_rate = self.exchange_performance[exchange_id].get("success_rate", 0.0)
+                         reliability_scores[exchange_id] = success_rate * 100
+                 
+                 # Combine scores with weights
+                 weights = {
+                     "price": 0.4,
+                     "fee": 0.2,
+                     "latency": 0.2,
+                     "reliability": 0.2
+                 }
+                 
+                 for exchange_id in exchanges:
+                     # Initialize with default scores for any missing factors
+                     price_score = price_scores.get(exchange_id, 50)
+                     fee_score = fee_scores.get(exchange_id, 50)
+                     latency_score = latency_scores.get(exchange_id, 50)
+                     reliability_score = reliability_scores.get(exchange_id, 50)
+                     
+                     # Calculate weighted score
+                     exchange_scores[exchange_id] = (
+                         price_score * weights["price"] +
+                         fee_score * weights["fee"] +
+                         latency_score * weights["latency"] +
+                         reliability_score * weights["reliability"]
+                     )
+             
+             # Default scores if no specific scoring was done
+             if not exchange_scores:
+                 for exchange_id in exchanges:
+                     exchange_scores[exchange_id] = 50.0
+             
+             # Find the best exchange
+             if not exchange_scores:
+                 logger.warning(f"No exchanges could be scored for {symbol}")
+                 return None
+             
+             best_exchange = max(exchange_scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1])
+             best_exchange_id = best_exchange[0]
+             best_score = best_exchange[1]
+             
+             # Create routing decision
+             decision = RoutingDecision(
+                 exchange_id=best_exchange_id,
+                 symbol=symbol,
+                 decision_time=datetime.utcnow(),
+                 primary_criterion=routing_criteria,
+                 score=best_score,
+                 alternatives=exchange_scores
+             )
+             
+             # Record the decision
+             self._record_routing_decision(decision)
+             
+             return decision
+     
+     def _record_routing_decision(self, decision: RoutingDecision):
+         """Record a routing decision in history and metrics."""
+         with self._lock:
+             # Add to history, maintaining max size
+             self.routing_history.append(decision)
+             if len(self.routing_history) > self.max_history_size:
+                 self.routing_history.pop(0)
+             
+             # Record metrics
+             if self.obs_manager:
+                 if self.metrics.get("routing_decisions"):
+                     self.metrics["routing_decisions"].labels(
+                         exchange=decision.exchange_id,
+                         criterion=decision.primary_criterion.value,
+                         symbol=decision.symbol
+                     ).inc()
+                 
+                 if self.metrics.get("routing_score"):
+                     self.metrics["routing_score"].labels(
+                         exchange=decision.exchange_id,
+                         criterion=decision.primary_criterion.value
+                     ).observe(decision.score)
+     
+     def get_routing_history(
+         self,
+         symbol: str = None,
+         exchange_id: str = None,
+         criterion: RoutingCriteria = None,
+         max_age_minutes: int = None
+     ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
+         """
+         Get routing decision history with optional filtering.
+         
+         Args:
+             symbol: Filter by symbol
+             exchange_id: Filter by exchange
+             criterion: Filter by routing criterion
+             max_age_minutes: Filter by decision age in minutes
+             
+         Returns:
+             List of routing decisions as dictionaries
+         """
+         with self._lock:
+             filtered_history = []
+             
+             for decision in self.routing_history:
+                 # Apply filters
+                 if symbol and decision.symbol != symbol:
+                     continue
+                 
+                 if exchange_id and decision.exchange_id != exchange_id:
+                     continue
+                 
+                 if criterion and decision.primary_criterion != criterion:
+                     continue
+                 
+                 if max_age_minutes:
+                     age = datetime.utcnow() - decision.decision_time
+                     if age > timedelta(minutes=max_age_minutes):
+                         continue
+                 
+                 filtered_history.append(decision.to_dict())
+             
+             return filtered_history
+     
+     def get_exchange_performance_summary(self) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
+         """
+         Get a summary of exchange performance statistics.
+         
+         Returns:
+             Dictionary mapping exchange IDs to performance metrics
+         """
+         with self._lock:
+             result = {}
+             
+             for exchange_id, perf in self.exchange_performance.items():
+                 result[exchange_id] = perf.copy()
+             
+             return result
+     
+     def get_price_data(self, symbol: str) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
+         """
+         Get current price data for a symbol across all exchanges.
+         
+         Args:
+             symbol: Trading symbol
+             
+         Returns:
+             Dictionary mapping exchange IDs to price data
+         """
+         with self._lock:
+             result = {}
+             
+             if symbol in self.price_cache:
+                 for exchange_id, price_data in self.price_cache[symbol].items():
+                     if price_data.is_fresh():
+                         result[exchange_id] = {
+                             "bid": price_data.bid,
+                             "ask": price_data.ask,
+                             "last": price_data.last,
+                             "mid": price_data.get_mid_price(),
+                             "bid_liquidity": price_data.bid_liquidity,
+                             "ask_liquidity": price_data.ask_liquidity,
+                             "timestamp": price_data.timestamp.isoformat()
+                         }
+             
+             return result 
```

Now, let's create the Exchange Analytics module to measure and report on the performance of exchanges:
