  """
  Exchange Profiler
- ---------------
- Analyzes and catalogs the capabilities, latency, and reliability of each exchange.
+ 
+ This module is responsible for analyzing and cataloging exchange capabilities,
+ latency, and reliability. It maintains a registry of exchanges and their
+ performance characteristics to help optimize trading across different platforms.
+ 
+ Key features:
+ - Exchange capability tracking
+ - Latency monitoring
+ - Reliability metrics
+ - Real-time exchange status
  """

+     """Represents the capabilities of a specific exchange"""
+     exchange_id: str
+     name: str
+     description: str
+     features: Set[ExchangeFeature] = field(default_factory=set)
+     supported_pairs: Set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
+     rate_limits: Optional[RateLimit] = None
+     fee_structure: Optional[FeeStructure] = None
+     min_order_size: Dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
+     precision: Dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
+     api_doc_url: str = ""
+     last_updated: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
+     
+     def has_feature(self, feature: ExchangeFeature) -> bool:
+         """Check if the exchange supports a specific feature"""
+         return feature in self.features
+     
+     def supports_symbol(self, symbol: str) -> bool:
+         """Check if the exchange supports a specific trading pair"""
+         return symbol in self.supported_pairs
+     
+     def get_min_order_size(self, symbol: str) -> Optional[float]:
+         """Get minimum order size for a symbol"""
+         return self.min_order_size.get(symbol)
+     
+     def get_precision(self, symbol: str) -> Optional[int]:
+         """Get decimal precision for a symbol"""
+         return self.precision.get(symbol)
+     
+     def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+         """Convert the capabilities to a dictionary"""
+         return {
+             "exchange_id": self.exchange_id,
+             "name": self.name,
+             "description": self.description,
+             "features": [f.value for f in self.features],
+             "supported_pairs": list(self.supported_pairs),
+             "rate_limits": self.rate_limits.__dict__ if self.rate_limits else None,
+             "fee_structure": self.fee_structure.__dict__ if self.fee_structure else None,
+             "min_order_size": self.min_order_size,
+             "precision": self.precision,
+             "api_doc_url": self.api_doc_url,
+             "last_updated": self.last_updated.isoformat()
+         }
+     
+     @classmethod
+     def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> 'ExchangeCapabilities':
+         """Create an instance from a dictionary"""
+         capabilities = cls(
+             exchange_id=data['exchange_id'],
+             name=data['name'],
+             description=data['description'],
+             api_doc_url=data.get('api_doc_url', ''),
+             last_updated=datetime.fromisoformat(data.get('last_updated', datetime.now().isoformat()))
+         )
+         
+         # Set features
+         for feature in data.get('features', []):
+             try:
+                 capabilities.features.add(ExchangeFeature(feature))
+             except ValueError:
+                 logger.warning(f"Unknown exchange feature: {feature}")
+                 
+         capabilities.supported_pairs = set(data.get('supported_pairs', []))
+         capabilities.min_order_size = data.get('min_order_size', {})
+         capabilities.precision = data.get('precision', {})
+         
+         # Set rate limits if present
+         if rate_limit_data := data.get('rate_limits'):
+             capabilities.rate_limits = RateLimit(**rate_limit_data)
+             
+         # Set fee structure if present
+         if fee_data := data.get('fee_structure'):
+             capabilities.fee_structure = FeeStructure(**fee_data)
+             
+         return capabilities
+

+     def get_reliability_score(self) -> float:
+         """Get a reliability score from 0 to 1 for the exchange"""
+         if self.total_requests == 0:
+             return 0.5  # Neutral score if no data
+         
+         success_rate = self.success_requests / self.total_requests
+         
+         # Weight more serious errors more heavily
+         timeout_weight = 0.5
+         timeout_penalty = (self.timeouts / self.total_requests) * timeout_weight if self.total_requests > 0 else 0
+         
+         # Calculate final score, ensuring it's between 0 and 1
+         score = max(0.0, min(1.0, success_rate - timeout_penalty))
+         return score
+

+             Dict with complete execution plan

+             goal=goal,
+             market_state=market_state,
+             size=size
+         )
+         
+         if not best_order_type:
+             logger.warning(f"Could not determine best order type for {exchange_id} with goal {goal.value}")
+             # Fall back to market order
+             best_order_type = {
+                 "order_type": "market",
+                 "parameters": {},
+                 "description": "Fallback market order",
+                 "score": 0
+             }
+         
+         # Base price calculation
+         mid_price = market_state.get("mid_price", 0)
+         best_bid = market_state.get("best_bid", 0)
+         best_ask = market_state.get("best_ask", 0)
+         
+         if not (mid_price and best_bid and best_ask):
+             logger.warning("Missing price information in market state")
+             return {
+                 "success": False,
+                 "error": "Missing price information"
+             }
+         
+         price = None
+         time_in_force = "GTC"  # Default
+         
+         # Determine price based on order type and side
+         if best_order_type["order_type"] == "market":
+             price = None  # Market orders don't need a price
+         else:
+             # Extract price improvement from parameters
+             price_improvement_ticks = best_order_type["parameters"].get("price_improvement_ticks", 0)
+             tick_size = market_state.get("tick_size", 0.01)
+             
+             if side == "buy":
+                 # For buy orders, price improvement means lower price (negative ticks)
+                 price = best_ask + (price_improvement_ticks * tick_size)
+                 
+                 # Apply max slippage if specified
+                 if max_slippage_bps:
+                     max_price = best_ask * (1 + max_slippage_bps / 10000)
+                     price = min(price, max_price)
+             else:  # sell
+                 # For sell orders, price improvement means higher price (positive ticks)
+                 price = best_bid + (price_improvement_ticks * tick_size)
+                 
+                 # Apply max slippage if specified
+                 if max_slippage_bps:
+                     min_price = best_bid * (1 - max_slippage_bps / 10000)
+                     price = max(price, min_price)
+         
+         # Get time in force from parameters
+         if "time_in_force" in best_order_type["parameters"]:
+             time_in_force = best_order_type["parameters"]["time_in_force"]
+         
+         # Special handling for advanced order types
+         if best_order_type["order_type"] == "twap":
+             # For TWAP, we need to calculate slice sizes
+             num_slices = best_order_type["parameters"].get("num_slices", 10)
+             interval_minutes = best_order_type["parameters"].get("interval_minutes", 3)
+             
+             slice_size = size / num_slices
+             
+             return {
+                 "success": True,
+                 "order_type": "twap",
+                 "num_slices": num_slices,
+                 "slice_size": slice_size,
+                 "interval_minutes": interval_minutes,
+                 "symbol": symbol,
+                 "side": side,
+                 "price": price,  # Base price, may be adjusted for each slice
+                 "total_size": size,
+                 "time_in_force": time_in_force,
+                 "estimated_duration_minutes": num_slices * interval_minutes,
+                 "reason": best_order_type["description"]
+             }
+         
+         elif best_order_type["order_type"] == "iceberg":
+             # For iceberg, calculate visible size
+             display_size_pct = best_order_type["parameters"].get("display_size_pct", 0.1)
+             visible_size = size * display_size_pct
+             
+             return {
+                 "success": True,
+                 "order_type": "iceberg",
+                 "symbol": symbol,
+                 "side": side,
+                 "price": price,
+                 "total_size": size,
+                 "visible_size": visible_size,
+                 "time_in_force": time_in_force,
+                 "reason": best_order_type["description"]
+             }
+         
+         # Standard order types
+         return {
+             "success": True,
+             "order_type": best_order_type["order_type"],
+             "symbol": symbol,
+             "side": side,
+             "price": price,
+             "size": size,
+             "time_in_force": time_in_force,
+             "additional_params": best_order_type["parameters"],
+             "reason": best_order_type["description"]
+         }

+     def _calculate_price_scores(self, symbol: str, side: str, exchanges: List[str]) -> Dict[str, float]:
+         """Calculate scores based on price comparison"""
+         scores = {}
+         best_exchange, best_price, _ = self.get_best_price(symbol, side, exchanges)
+         
+         if best_exchange and best_price > 0:
+             scores[best_exchange] = 100.0
+             
+             for exchange_id in exchanges:
+                 if exchange_id != best_exchange and symbol in self.price_cache and exchange_id in self.price_cache[symbol]:
+                     price_data = self.price_cache[symbol][exchange_id]
+                     
+                     if price_data.is_fresh():
+                         exchange_price = price_data.ask if side == "buy" else price_data.bid
+                         
+                         if exchange_price > 0:
+                             # Calculate price difference in basis points
+                             if side == "buy":
+                                 # Lower is better for buys
+                                 price_diff_bps = (exchange_price - best_price) / best_price * 10000
+                                 scores[exchange_id] = max(0, 100 - price_diff_bps)
+                             else:
+                                 # Higher is better for sells
+                                 price_diff_bps = (best_price - exchange_price) / best_price * 10000
+                                 scores[exchange_id] = max(0, 100 - price_diff_bps)
+         
+         return scores
+     
+     def _calculate_fee_scores(self, exchanges: List[str]) -> Dict[str, float]:
+         """Calculate scores based on fee comparison"""
+         scores = {}
+         lowest_fee = float('inf')
+         
+         # Find lowest fee
+         for exchange_id in exchanges:
+             fee = float('inf')
+             
+             if self.exchange_profiler:
+                 capabilities = self.exchange_profiler.get_capabilities(exchange_id)
+                 if capabilities and capabilities.fee_structure:
+                     fee = capabilities.fee_structure.taker_fee
+             
+             if fee < lowest_fee:
+                 lowest_fee = fee
+         
+         # Score based on fee comparison
+         if lowest_fee < float('inf'):
+             for exchange_id in exchanges:
+                 fee = float('inf')
+                 
+                 if self.exchange_profiler:
+                     capabilities = self.exchange_profiler.get_capabilities(exchange_id)
+                     if capabilities and capabilities.fee_structure:
+                         fee = capabilities.fee_structure.taker_fee
+                 
+                 if fee < float('inf'):
+                     scores[exchange_id] = min(100, (lowest_fee / fee) * 100)
+                 else:
+                     scores[exchange_id] = 50  # Default for unknown fee
+         
+         return scores
+     
+     def _calculate_latency_scores(self, exchanges: List[str]) -> Dict[str, float]:
+         """Calculate scores based on latency comparison"""
+         scores = {}
+         lowest_latency = float('inf')
+         
+         # Find lowest latency
+         for exchange_id in exchanges:
+             latency = float('inf')
+             
+             if exchange_id in self.exchange_performance:
+                 latency = self.exchange_performance[exchange_id].get("avg_latency_ms", float('inf'))
+             elif self.exchange_profiler:
+                 metrics = self.exchange_profiler.get_performance_metrics(exchange_id)
+                 if metrics:
+                     latency = metrics.get_avg_latency("order_submit") or metrics.get_avg_latency()
+             
+             if latency < lowest_latency and latency > 0:
+                 lowest_latency = latency
+         
+         # Score based on latency comparison
+         if lowest_latency < float('inf'):
+             for exchange_id in exchanges:
+                 latency = float('inf')
+                 
+                 if exchange_id in self.exchange_performance:
+                     latency = self.exchange_performance[exchange_id].get("avg_latency_ms", float('inf'))
+                 elif self.exchange_profiler:
+                     metrics = self.exchange_profiler.get_performance_metrics(exchange_id)
+                     if metrics:
+                         latency = metrics.get_avg_latency("order_submit") or metrics.get_avg_latency()
+                 
+                 if latency < float('inf') and latency > 0:
+                     scores[exchange_id] = min(100, max(10, (lowest_latency / latency) * 100))
+                 else:
+                     scores[exchange_id] = 50  # Default for unknown latency
+         
+         return scores
+     
+     def _calculate_reliability_scores(self, exchanges: List[str]) -> Dict[str, float]:
+         """Calculate scores based on reliability comparison"""
+         scores = {}
+         
+         for exchange_id in exchanges:
+             reliability = 0.0
+             
+             if self.exchange_profiler:
+                 reliability = self.exchange_profiler.get_reliability_score(exchange_id)
+             elif exchange_id in self.exchange_performance:
+                 reliability = self.exchange_performance[exchange_id].get("success_rate", 0.0)
+             
+             scores[exchange_id] = reliability * 100
+         
+         return scores

+     
+     def route_multi_exchange_order(
+         self,
+         symbol: str,
+         side: str,
+         size: float,
+         num_exchanges: int = 2,
+         routing_criteria: RoutingCriteria = RoutingCriteria.SMART,
+         min_allocation_pct: float = 0.1
+     ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
+         """
+         Route an order across multiple exchanges for better execution.
+         
+         Args:
+             symbol: Trading symbol
+             side: Order side ("buy" or "sell")
+             size: Total order size
+             num_exchanges: Number of exchanges to use
+             routing_criteria: Criteria for exchange selection
+             min_allocation_pct: Minimum percentage of total size per exchange
+             
+         Returns:
+             List of allocation dictionaries with exchange_id, size, and pct
+         """
+         # Get candidate exchanges
+         exchanges = self.get_exchanges_for_symbol(symbol)
+         
+         if not exchanges:
+             logger.warning(f"No exchanges available for symbol: {symbol}")
+             return []
+         
+         # Limit number of exchanges
+         num_exchanges = min(len(exchanges), num_exchanges)
+         
+         if num_exchanges == 1:
+             # Just use a single exchange
+             return [{
+                 "exchange_id": exchanges[0],
+                 "size": size,
+                 "percentage": 1.0
+             }]
+         
+         # Route to get ranked exchanges
+         all_decisions = []
+         remaining_exchanges = exchanges.copy()
+         
+         for _ in range(num_exchanges):
+             if not remaining_exchanges:
+                 break
+                 
+             decision = self.route_order(
+                 symbol=symbol,
+                 side=side,
+                 size=size / num_exchanges,  # Approximate size per exchange
+                 routing_criteria=routing_criteria,
+                 exchanges=remaining_exchanges
+             )
+             
+             if decision:
+                 all_decisions.append(decision)
+                 remaining_exchanges.remove(decision.exchange_id)
+             else:
+                 break
+         
+         if not all_decisions:
+             logger.warning("Could not find suitable exchanges for multi-exchange order")
+             return []
+         
+         # Calculate scores and normalize to percentages
+         exchange_scores = {}
+         total_score = 0
+         
+         for decision in all_decisions:
+             exchange_scores[decision.exchange_id] = decision.score
+             total_score += decision.score
+         
+         if total_score == 0:
+             # Equal allocation if all scores are 0
+             equal_pct = 1.0 / len(all_decisions)
+             allocations = [
+                 {
+                     "exchange_id": decision.exchange_id,
+                     "percentage": equal_pct,
+                     "size": size * equal_pct
+                 }
+                 for decision in all_decisions
+             ]
+         else:
+             # Allocate based on scores
+             raw_allocations = {
+                 exchange_id: score / total_score
+                 for exchange_id, score in exchange_scores.items()
+             }
+             
+             # Ensure minimum allocation percentages
+             min_allocation = min_allocation_pct
+             
+             # Adjust if minimum allocation is too high
+             if min_allocation * len(raw_allocations) > 1.0:
+                 min_allocation = 1.0 / len(raw_allocations)
+             
+             # Apply minimum allocation logic
+             adjusted_allocations = {}
+             remaining_allocation = 1.0
+             remaining_exchanges = list(raw_allocations.keys())
+             
+             # First, allocate minimums to all exchanges
+             for exchange_id in raw_allocations:
+                 adjusted_allocations[exchange_id] = min_allocation
+                 remaining_allocation -= min_allocation
+             
+             # Then distribute remaining allocation proportionally
+             if remaining_allocation > 0 and remaining_exchanges:
+                 # Recalculate proportions for the remaining allocation
+                 remaining_proportions = {}
+                 remaining_score_total = sum(exchange_scores[ex] for ex in remaining_exchanges)
+                 
+                 if remaining_score_total > 0:
+                     for exchange_id in remaining_exchanges:
+                         proportion = exchange_scores[exchange_id] / remaining_score_total
+                         remaining_proportions[exchange_id] = proportion
+                     
+                     # Allocate the remaining amount
+                     for exchange_id, proportion in remaining_proportions.items():
+                         additional = remaining_allocation * proportion
+                         adjusted_allocations[exchange_id] += additional
+             
+             # Create allocation objects
+             allocations = [
+                 {
+                     "exchange_id": exchange_id,
+                     "percentage": percentage,
+                     "size": size * percentage
+                 }
+                 for exchange_id, percentage in adjusted_allocations.items()
+             ]
+         
+         # Sort by size descending
+         allocations.sort(key=lambda x: x["size"], reverse=True)
+         
+         return allocations
+     
+     def update_all_price_data(self) -> None:
+         """
+         Update all price data by querying exchange profiler and/or external sources.
+         This method should be called periodically to refresh price information.
+         """
+         # Skip if no exchange profiler available
+         if not self.exchange_profiler:
+             logger.warning("No exchange profiler available to update price data")
+             return
+         
+         # Get all symbols across all exchanges
+         all_symbols = set()
+         for symbol_exchanges in self.symbol_exchanges.values():
+             all_symbols.update(symbol_exchanges)
+         
+         # Additional symbols from price cache
+         all_symbols.update(self.price_cache.keys())
+         
+         # Update each symbol
+         for symbol in all_symbols:
+             # Get exchanges for this symbol
+             exchanges = self.get_exchanges_for_symbol(symbol)
+             
+             for exchange_id in exchanges:
+                 try:
+                     # Use exchange profiler to get current price data
+                     # This would typically call the exchange API
+                     if self.exchange_profiler:
+                         # This is a placeholder - in a real implementation,
+                         # you would get actual price data from the exchange
+                         # through the exchange profiler
+                         pass
+                 except Exception as e:
+                     logger.error(f"Error updating price data for {symbol} on {exchange_id}: {e}")
+     
+     def clear_cache(self) -> None:
+         """Clear price and routing caches"""
+         self.price_cache.clear()
+         self.routing_history.clear()
+         logger.debug("Cleared router caches") 
```

  The system implements several performance optimization techniques:

  - **Cache compression**: Reduces memory footprint for large datasets
- - **Future Enhancement**: Redis integration for distributed caching
- 
- ### Visualization Optimization
- - **Lazy loading**: Components load only when needed
- - **Downsampling**: Large datasets are intelligently reduced for visualization
- - **DOM optimizations**: Minimizes browser reflows and repaints
- - **Future Enhancement**: Progressive loading for large datasets
+ 
+ ### Low-Latency Processing
+ - **Critical paths optimized** for minimal latency
+ - **Efficient data structures** for high-throughput operations
+ - **Asynchronous processing** for non-critical operations
  
  ### Real-time Updates
  - **Selective updates**: Only changed data is refreshed
  - **Update prioritization**: Market data > Performance metrics > Analysis
- - **Future Enhancement**: WebSocket connections for true real-time data

# Instinct AI Trading System: User Guide

## 1. Introduction

### Purpose and Vision

The Instinct AI Trading System is our comprehensive cryptocurrency trading platform designed to identify and capitalize on market inefficiencies across multiple exchanges. This isn't just another trading system - it's a sophisticated market participant engineered to execute complex strategies with precision and adaptability.

### Core Capabilities

- **Multi-Strategy Execution**: Simultaneously run statistical arbitrage, funding rate arbitrage, and algorithmic trading strategies
- **Exchange Optimization**: Intelligently route orders across exchanges for optimal execution
- **Market Microstructure Analysis**: Leverage order book dynamics for short-term price movement prediction
- **Adaptive Risk Management**: Dynamically adjust position sizing and risk parameters based on market conditions

### Performance Targets

- Target annual returns: 15-100%
- Sharpe ratio target: >2.0
- Maximum drawdown tolerance: <20%
- Strategy diversification: Minimum 3 uncorrelated strategy types

## 2. System Setup and Configuration

### Initial Setup

```bash
# Clone the repository
git clone https://github.com/your-username/instinct-ai-trading.git
cd instinct-ai-trading

# Set up virtual environment
python -m venv venv
source venv/bin/activate  # On Windows: venv\Scripts\activate

# Install dependencies
pip install -r requirements.txt

# Initialize the system
python setup.py
```

### Configuration Files

The system uses a hierarchy of configuration files:

1. **`config/default.yaml`**: Base configuration, never modify directly
2. **`config/user.yaml`**: Your personal settings that override defaults
3. **`config/exchanges.yaml`**: Exchange API credentials and settings
4. **`config/strategies.yaml`**: Strategy-specific parameters

Example user configuration:
```yaml
# config/user.yaml
system:
  name: "Instinct AI"
  log_level: "INFO"
  data_directory: "/path/to/data"

risk:
  max_position_size: 10000  # USD
  max_open_positions: 5
  max_drawdown_pct: 15

notifications:
  enable_email: true
  email_address: "your-email@example.com"
  enable_telegram: false
```

### Exchange Setup

To add a new exchange:

1. Edit `config/exchanges.yaml`:
```yaml
exchanges:
  binance:
    api_key: "YOUR_API_KEY"
    api_secret: "YOUR_API_SECRET"
    testnet: false
    enabled: true
    
  coinbase:
    api_key: "YOUR_API_KEY"
    api_secret: "YOUR_API_SECRET"
    enabled: true
```

2. Run the exchange verification tool:
```bash
python tools/verify_exchange_access.py
```

3. Initialize exchange profiles:
```bash
python -m advanced_trading.exchange_optimization.tools.profile_exchanges
```

## 3. Daily Operations

### Starting the System

```bash
# Start the main system
python run.py --mode production

# Start in simulation mode (no real trades)
python run.py --mode simulation

# Start with specific strategies only
python run.py --strategies funding_arb,stat_arb
```

### Dashboard Access

Once running, access the dashboard at: `http://localhost:8050`

The dashboard provides:
- Real-time strategy performance
- Open positions and orders
- Exchange status monitoring
- Market alerts and signals
- System health metrics

### Monitoring and Alerts

Key metrics to monitor daily:
- Strategy PnL (overall and per-strategy)
- Position sizes and exposure
- Exchange performance (latency, errors)
- Market condition indicators
- System resource usage

Configure alerts in `config/alerts.yaml`:
```yaml
alerts:
  drawdown:
    threshold_pct: 5
    channels: ["email", "dashboard"]
  
  exchange_error:
    consecutive_count: 3
    channels: ["dashboard", "log"]
```

### Stopping the System

To safely stop the system:
```bash
# Graceful shutdown (completes open orders first)
python run.py --shutdown graceful

# Emergency shutdown (cancels all open orders)
python run.py --shutdown emergency
```

## 4. Strategy Configuration and Management

### Available Strategy Types

1. **Statistical Arbitrage**
   - Purpose: Exploits price differences between correlated assets
   - Configuration: Set correlation thresholds, position limits, and pair selection
   - Location: `advanced_trading/strategies/statistical_arbitrage.py`

2. **Funding Rate Arbitrage**
   - Purpose: Capitalizes on funding rate differentials in perpetual futures
   - Configuration: Set minimum rate thresholds, holding periods, and position sizing
   - Location: `advanced_trading/strategies/funding_rate_arbitrage.py`

3. **Market Microstructure Strategies**
   - Purpose: Leverages order book imbalances for short-term price predictions
   - Configuration: Set order book depth, signal thresholds, and execution speed
   - Location: `advanced_trading/strategies/microstructure_strategy.py`

### Configuring a Strategy

Edit `config/strategies.yaml` to configure strategy parameters:

```yaml
strategies:
  stat_arb_btc_eth:
    type: "statistical_arbitrage"
    enabled: true
    pairs: ["BTC/USD", "ETH/USD"]
    correlation_threshold: 0.7
    z_score_entry: 2.0
    z_score_exit: 0.5
    max_position_usd: 5000
    exchanges: ["binance", "coinbase"]
    
  funding_arb_1:
    type: "funding_rate_arbitrage"
    enabled: true
    markets: ["BTC-PERP"]
    min_funding_rate: 0.01
    max_position_usd: 10000
    target_exchanges: ["binance", "ftx"]
```

### Activating and Deactivating Strategies

To enable/disable a strategy without restarting the system:

```bash
# Enable a strategy
python tools/strategy_control.py --enable stat_arb_btc_eth

# Disable a strategy
python tools/strategy_control.py --disable funding_arb_1

# List active strategies
python tools/strategy_control.py --list
```

## 5. Using the Exchange-Specific Optimization Module

### Smart Order Routing

The system automatically routes orders to the optimal exchange based on your preferences:

```yaml
# config/routing.yaml
default_routing:
  criterion: "SMART"  # Options: SMART, LOWEST_FEES, FASTEST_EXECUTION, BEST_PRICE
  require_price_data: true
  
exchange_weights:
  latency: 0.3
  fees: 0.3
  reliability: 0.2
  price: 0.2
  
# Strategy-specific routing overrides
strategy_routing:
  stat_arb_btc_eth:
    criterion: "BEST_PRICE"
```

### Monitoring Order Routing Decisions

View routing decisions in the dashboard or via CLI:

```bash
# View recent routing decisions
python tools/show_routing.py --last 20

# View routing decisions for a specific symbol
python tools/show_routing.py --symbol BTC/USD

# Export routing analytics
python tools/show_routing.py --export routing_analysis.csv
```

### Exchange Performance Analytics

Monitor and compare exchange performance:

```bash
# Generate exchange performance report
python -m advanced_trading.exchange_optimization.tools.exchange_report

# Compare execution quality across exchanges
python tools/compare_exchanges.py --metric latency --period 7d
```

## 6. Risk Management

### Position Limits

Configure global and per-strategy position limits:

```yaml
# config/risk.yaml
global:
  max_portfolio_value: 100000  # USD
  max_exposure_per_asset: 0.2  # 20% of portfolio
  max_drawdown_pct: 15

per_strategy:
  statistical_arbitrage:
    max_position_usd: 10000
    max_pairs: 5
    
  funding_rate_arbitrage:
    max_position_usd: 20000
    max_exchanges: 3
```

### Volatility-Based Adjustments

The system automatically adjusts position sizes based on market volatility:

```yaml
# config/risk_adjustment.yaml
volatility_scaling:
  enabled: true
  lookback_days: 10
  target_volatility: 0.15
  max_leverage: 2.0
```

### Circuit Breakers

Configure circuit breakers to protect against extreme market conditions:

```yaml
# config/circuit_breakers.yaml
global_breakers:
  - type: "drawdown"
    threshold_pct: 10
    action: "reduce_positions"
    
  - type: "volatility"
    threshold_pct: 50
    action: "pause_strategies"
```

## 7. Performance Analysis and Optimization

### Performance Metrics

Key metrics to evaluate system performance:

- **Total Return**: Absolute and percentage returns
- **Sharpe Ratio**: Risk-adjusted performance
- **Maximum Drawdown**: Largest peak-to-trough decline
- **Win Rate**: Percentage of profitable trades
- **Profit Factor**: Gross profits divided by gross losses
- **Exchange Costs**: Trading fees as percentage of PnL

### Performance Analysis Tools

```bash
# Generate daily performance report
python tools/performance_report.py --period daily

# Compare strategy performance
python tools/strategy_comparison.py --lookback 30d

# Analyze execution quality
python tools/execution_analysis.py --strategy funding_arb_1
```

### Strategy Optimization

The system includes tools for strategy parameter optimization:

```bash
# Run optimization for a strategy
python tools/optimize_strategy.py --strategy stat_arb_btc_eth --parameter z_score_entry --range 1.5,3.0,0.1

# Apply optimized parameters
python tools/optimize_strategy.py --apply optimization_results.json
```

## 8. System Maintenance

### Data Management

Manage historical data:

```bash
# Download historical data
python tools/data_manager.py --download --symbols BTC/USD,ETH/USD --timeframe 1h --days 90

# Clean and verify data
python tools/data_manager.py --validate --repair
```

### System Backup

Regular system backups:

```bash
# Backup configuration and database
python tools/backup.py --config --database

# Restore from backup
python tools/backup.py --restore backup_20230415.zip
```

### Update Procedures

```bash
# Update the system to the latest version
git pull origin main
pip install -r requirements.txt
python tools/db_migrate.py
```

## 9. Troubleshooting

### Common Issues

| Issue | Potential Solution |
|-------|-------------------|
| Exchange connection timeout | Check API keys, network connection, and exchange status |
| Strategy not executing trades | Verify strategy parameters, availability of funds, and exchange permissions |
| Excessive slippage | Adjust execution parameters, use exchange optimization module |
| System performance degradation | Check system resources, optimize database, prune old data |

### Logs and Diagnostics

```bash
# View system logs
tail -f logs/system.log

# View strategy-specific logs
tail -f logs/strategies/stat_arb_btc_eth.log

# Generate diagnostic report
python tools/diagnostics.py --full
```

## 10. Advanced Features

### Custom Strategy Development

To create a custom strategy:

1. Create a new strategy class:
```python
# advanced_trading/strategies/my_custom_strategy.py
from advanced_trading.strategies.base_strategy import BaseStrategy

class MyCustomStrategy(BaseStrategy):
    def __init__(self, config):
        super().__init__(config)
        # Initialize strategy-specific parameters
        
    def generate_signals(self, market_data):
        # Implement signal generation logic
        return signals
        
    def manage_positions(self, positions, signals):
        # Implement position management logic
        return orders
```

2. Register the strategy:
```python
# advanced_trading/strategies/__init__.py
from .my_custom_strategy import MyCustomStrategy

STRATEGY_REGISTRY["my_custom"] = MyCustomStrategy
```

3. Configure the strategy:
```yaml
# config/strategies.yaml
strategies:
  my_custom_strategy_1:
    type: "my_custom"
    enabled: true
    # Strategy-specific parameters
```

+ 1. Create a new strategy class:
+ ```python
+ # advanced_trading/strategies/my_custom_strategy.py
+ from advanced_trading.strategies.base_strategy import BaseStrategy
+ 
+ class MyCustomStrategy(BaseStrategy):
+     def __init__(self, config):
+         super().__init__(config)
+         # Initialize strategy-specific parameters
+         
+     def generate_signals(self, market_data):
+         # Implement signal generation logic
+         return signals
+         
+     def manage_positions(self, positions, signals):
+         # Implement position management logic
+         return orders
+ ```
+ 
+ 2. Register the strategy:
+ ```python
+ # advanced_trading/strategies/__init__.py
+ from .my_custom_strategy import MyCustomStrategy
+ 
+ STRATEGY_REGISTRY["my_custom"] = MyCustomStrategy
+ ```
+ 
+ 3. Configure the strategy:
+ ```yaml
+ # config/strategies.yaml
+ strategies:
+   my_custom_strategy_1:
+     type: "my_custom"
+     enabled: true
+     # Strategy-specific parameters
+ ```
+ 
+ ## 11. Market Microstructure Analysis
+ 
+ ### Using Order Book Analysis
+ 
+ The Market Microstructure Analysis module provides deep insights into order book dynamics:
+ 
+ ```python
+ # Example usage in a custom strategy
+ from advanced_trading.market_microstructure import OrderBookAnalyzer
+ 
+ # Initialize analyzer
+ analyzer = OrderBookAnalyzer(
+     depth=10,  # Analyze top 10 levels
+     update_interval_ms=100
+ )
+ 
+ # Get order book metrics
+ imbalance = analyzer.get_order_book_imbalance("BTC/USD")
+ depth_profile = analyzer.get_liquidity_profile("ETH/USD")
+ price_pressure = analyzer.calculate_price_pressure("BTC/USD", size=1.0)
+ 
+ # Make trading decisions based on microstructure
+ if imbalance > 0.7 and price_pressure < 0.05:
+     # Execute buy strategy
+ ```
+ 
+ ### Order Flow Analysis
+ 
+ Detect and capitalize on order flow imbalances:
+ 
+ ```yaml
+ # config/microstructure.yaml
+ order_flow:
+   enabled: true
+   window_size: 20
+   impact_threshold: 0.65
+   decay_factor: 0.95
+   symbols: ["BTC/USD", "ETH/USD"]
+ ```
+ 
+ ### Liquidity Profiling
+ 
+ Configure liquidity analysis:
+ 
+ ```yaml
+ # config/liquidity.yaml
+ liquidity_profiler:
+   price_levels: 20
+   update_interval_ms: 250
+   abnormal_threshold: 2.5
+   history_window: 100
+ ```
+ 
+ ## 12. Integration Testing
+ 
+ ### Testing Exchange Connectivity
+ 
+ Verify exchange connections:
+ 
+ ```bash
+ # Test all configured exchanges
+ python tools/test_exchanges.py
+ 
+ # Test specific exchange functions
+ python tools/test_exchanges.py --exchange binance --functions order_book,recent_trades
+ ```
+ 
+ ### Strategy Backtesting
+ 
+ Test strategies before deployment:
+ 
+ ```bash
+ # Backtest a strategy
+ python tools/backtest.py --strategy stat_arb_btc_eth --start-date 2023-01-01 --end-date 2023-02-01
+ 
+ # Backtest with specific parameters
+ python tools/backtest.py --strategy funding_arb_1 --params "min_funding_rate=0.005,max_position_usd=5000"
+ ```
+ 
+ ### System Integration Tests
+ 
+ Verify system component integration:
+ 
+ ```bash
+ # Run all integration tests
+ python -m tests.integration.run_all
+ 
+ # Test specific integration
+ python -m tests.integration.test_strategy_execution
+ ```
+ 
+ ## 13. Future Development Roadmap
+ 
+ Our ongoing development priorities:
+ 
+ 1. **Machine Learning Enhancements**
+    - Price prediction models
+    - Anomaly detection
+    - Adaptive parameter optimization
+ 
+ 2. **Advanced Execution Algorithms**
+    - Implementation of TWAP and VWAP algorithms
+    - Adaptive execution based on market conditions
+    - Iceberg and layered order strategies
+ 
+ 3. **Additional Strategy Types**
+    - Options-based volatility strategies
+    - Cross-exchange arbitrage enhancements
+    - Event-driven strategies
+ 
+ 4. **System Optimizations**
+    - Low-latency critical path improvements
+    - Database performance enhancements
+    - Resource usage optimizations
+ 
+ 5. **Dashboard Enhancements**
+    - Strategy performance visualization improvements
+    - Advanced market analytics displays
+    - Custom alert configuration interface
+

+ """
+ Integration Tests for Exchange Profiler
+ 
+ Tests the integration between ExchangeProfiler and other system components.
+ """
+ 
+ import unittest
+ import logging
+ from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch
+ from datetime import datetime, timedelta
+ 
+ from advanced_trading.exchange_optimization.profiles.exchange_profiler import (
+     ExchangeProfiler, ExchangeCapabilities, ExchangeStatus, ExchangeFeature
+ )
+ from advanced_trading.observability.metrics_manager import MetricsManager
+ from advanced_trading.data.exchange_data_provider import ExchangeDataProvider
+ 
+ # Configure logging
+ logging.basicConfig(level=logging.DEBUG)
+ logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
+ 
+ 
+ class MockExchangeAPI:
+     """Mock exchange API for testing"""
+     
+     def __init__(self, exchange_id, status="online"):
+         self.exchange_id = exchange_id
+         self.status = status
+         self.supported_symbols = [
+             "BTC/USD", "ETH/USD", "BTC/USDT", "ETH/USDT", "SOL/USD"
+         ]
+         self.features = [
+             "limit_orders", "market_orders", "stop_loss"
+         ]
+         
+     def get_capabilities(self):
+         """Return mock exchange capabilities"""
+         return {
+             "exchange_id": self.exchange_id,
+             "name": f"{self.exchange_id.capitalize()} Exchange",
+             "description": f"Mock {self.exchange_id} exchange for testing",
+             "features": self.features,
+             "supported_pairs": self.supported_symbols,
+             "rate_limits": {
+                 "requests_per_minute": 60,
+                 "requests_per_hour": 3000,
+                 "orders_per_minute": 10
+             },
+             "fee_structure": {
+                 "maker_fee": 0.001,
+                 "taker_fee": 0.002
+             }
+         }
+         
+     def get_status(self):
+         """Return mock exchange status"""
+         return self.status
+         
+     def get_time(self):
+         """Return server time"""
+         return datetime.utcnow()
+ 
+ 
+ class TestExchangeProfilerIntegration(unittest.TestCase):
+     """Test the integration of ExchangeProfiler with other components"""
+ 
+     def setUp(self):
+         """Set up the test environment"""
+         # Create mock metrics manager
+         self.metrics_manager = MagicMock(spec=MetricsManager)
+         
+         # Create mock data provider
+         self.data_provider = MagicMock(spec=ExchangeDataProvider)
+         
+         # Create exchange profiler with mock dependencies
+         self.profiler = ExchangeProfiler(
+             observability_manager=self.metrics_manager,
+             track_latency=True
+         )
+         
+         # Mock exchanges
+         self.mock_exchanges = {
+             "binance": MockExchangeAPI("binance"),
+             "coinbase": MockExchangeAPI("coinbase"),
+             "kraken": MockExchangeAPI("kraken", status="degraded")
+         }
+ 
+     def test_register_exchange_with_observability(self):
+         """Test that registering an exchange integrates with observability system"""
+         # Register an exchange
+         exchange_id = "binance"
+         mock_api = self.mock_exchanges[exchange_id]
+         capabilities_dict = mock_api.get_capabilities()
+         
+         # Register with profiler
+         self.profiler.register_exchange(exchange_id)
+         
+         # Set up capabilities using mock API data
+         capabilities = ExchangeCapabilities(
+             exchange_id=capabilities_dict["exchange_id"],
+             name=capabilities_dict["name"],
+             description=capabilities_dict["description"]
+         )
+         
+         # Add features
+         for feature_name in capabilities_dict["features"]:
+             try:
+                 capabilities.features.add(ExchangeFeature(feature_name))
+             except ValueError:
+                 pass
+         
+         # Update capabilities
+         self.profiler.update_capabilities(exchange_id, capabilities)
+         
+         # Update status
+         self.profiler.update_status(exchange_id, ExchangeStatus.ONLINE)
+         
+         # Verify metrics manager was called to record event
+         self.metrics_manager.record_event.assert_called_with(
+             "exchange_status_change",
+             {
+                 "exchange_id": exchange_id, 
+                 "old_status": None, 
+                 "new_status": "online"
+             }
+         )
+ 
+     def test_record_latency_integration(self):
+         """Test recording latency integrates with observability"""
+         exchange_id = "binance"
+         
+         # Register exchange
+         self.profiler.register_exchange(exchange_id)
+         
+         # Record some latency
+         self.profiler.record_latency(
+             exchange_id=exchange_id,
+             latency_ms=50.0,
+             operation_type="public_api"
+         )
+         
+         # Verify metrics were recorded
+         self.metrics_manager.record_metric.assert_called_with(
+             "exchange_latency",
+             50.0,
+             {"exchange_id": exchange_id, "operation_type": "public_api"}
+         )
+ 
+     def test_find_best_exchange_integration(self):
+         """Test finding the best exchange integrates with other components"""
+         # Register exchanges
+         for exchange_id, mock_api in self.mock_exchanges.items():
+             self.profiler.register_exchange(exchange_id)
+             
+             # Create capabilities
+             capabilities_dict = mock_api.get_capabilities()
+             capabilities = ExchangeCapabilities(
+                 exchange_id=capabilities_dict["exchange_id"],
+                 name=capabilities_dict["name"],
+                 description=capabilities_dict["description"]
+             )
+             
+             # Add symbols
+             for symbol in capabilities_dict["supported_pairs"]:
+                 capabilities.supported_pairs.add(symbol)
+                 
+             # Update capabilities
+             self.profiler.update_capabilities(exchange_id, capabilities)
+             
+             # Update status based on mock API
+             status = ExchangeStatus.ONLINE
+             if mock_api.status == "degraded":
+                 status = ExchangeStatus.DEGRADED
+             self.profiler.update_status(exchange_id, status)
+             
+             # Record some latency data
+             self.profiler.record_latency(
+                 exchange_id=exchange_id,
+                 latency_ms=30.0 + (10.0 * list(self.mock_exchanges.keys()).index(exchange_id)),
+                 operation_type="general"
+             )
+         
+         # Find best exchange for a symbol
+         best_exchange = self.profiler.find_best_exchange("BTC/USD", criterion="latency")
+         
+         # Should be binance (lowest latency in our setup)
+         self.assertEqual(best_exchange, "binance")
+ 
+     def test_data_provider_integration(self):
+         """Test integration with data provider for supported symbols"""
+         # Mock data provider to return symbols
+         symbol_map = {
+             "binance": ["BTC/USD", "ETH/USD", "LINK/USD"],
+             "coinbase": ["BTC/USD", "ETH/USD", "ADA/USD"],
+             "kraken": ["BTC/USD", "ETH/USD", "DOT/USD"]
+         }
+         
+         self.data_provider.get_supported_symbols.side_effect = lambda exchange_id: symbol_map.get(exchange_id, [])
+         
+         # Register exchanges
+         for exchange_id in symbol_map.keys():
+             self.profiler.register_exchange(exchange_id)
+             
+             # Get symbols from data provider
+             symbols = self.data_provider.get_supported_symbols(exchange_id)
+             
+             # Update exchange symbols in profiler
+             for symbol in symbols:
+                 with patch.object(self.profiler, 'symbol_exchanges', {}) as mock_symbol_map:
+                     if symbol not in mock_symbol_map:
+                         mock_symbol_map[symbol] = set()
+                     mock_symbol_map[symbol].add(exchange_id)
+         
+         # Test exchange lookup
+         with patch.object(self.profiler, 'symbol_exchanges', {
+             "BTC/USD": {"binance", "coinbase", "kraken"},
+             "ETH/USD": {"binance", "coinbase", "kraken"},
+             "LINK/USD": {"binance"},
+             "ADA/USD": {"coinbase"},
+             "DOT/USD": {"kraken"}
+         }):
+             # Get exchanges for LINK/USD (should only be binance)
+             exchanges = list(self.profiler.symbol_exchanges.get("LINK/USD", set()))
+             self.assertEqual(exchanges, ["binance"])
+             
+             # Get exchanges for BTC/USD (should be all three)
+             exchanges = self.profiler.symbol_exchanges.get("BTC/USD", set())
+             self.assertEqual(len(exchanges), 3)
+             self.assertIn("binance", exchanges)
+             self.assertIn("coinbase", exchanges)
+             self.assertIn("kraken", exchanges)
+ 
+     def test_reliability_scoring_integration(self):
+         """Test reliability scoring integrates with observability"""
+         exchange_id = "binance"
+         
+         # Register exchange
+         self.profiler.register_exchange(exchange_id)
+         
+         # Record some successes and failures
+         for i in range(10):
+             success = i < 8  # 8 successes, 2 failures
+             self.profiler.record_request_result(
+                 exchange_id=exchange_id,
+                 success=success,
+                 error_type="timeout" if not success else None
+             )
+         
+         # Get reliability score
+         score = self.profiler.get_reliability_score(exchange_id)
+         
+         # Verify score calculation and metrics recording
+         self.assertAlmostEqual(score, 0.7, places=1)  # ~70% reliability after penalties
+         self.metrics_manager.record_metric.assert_any_call(
+             "exchange_reliability",
+             score,
+             {"exchange_id": exchange_id}
+         )
+ 
+ 
+ if __name__ == "__main__":
+     unittest.main() 
```

+ """
+ Integration Tests for Smart Order Router
+ 
+ Tests the integration between SmartOrderRouter and other system components.
+ """
+ 
+ import unittest
+ import logging
+ from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch
+ from datetime import datetime, timedelta
+ 
+ from advanced_trading.exchange_optimization.routers.smart_order_router import (
+     SmartOrderRouter, RoutingCriteria, RoutingDecision, ExchangePriceData
+ )
+ from advanced_trading.exchange_optimization.profiles.exchange_profiler import (
+     ExchangeProfiler, ExchangeCapabilities, ExchangeStatus, ExchangeFeature
+ )
+ from advanced_trading.exchange_optimization.order_types.order_type_optimizer import (
+     OrderTypeOptimizer, OrderExecutionGoal
+ )
+ from advanced_trading.market_microstructure.analyzers.order_book_analyzer import OrderBookAnalyzer
+ from advanced_trading.market_microstructure.analyzers.liquidity_profiler import LiquidityProfiler
+ from advanced_trading.observability.metrics_manager import MetricsManager
+ 
+ # Configure logging
+ logging.basicConfig(level=logging.DEBUG)
+ logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
+ 
+ 
+ class TestSmartOrderRouterIntegration(unittest.TestCase):
+     """Test the integration of SmartOrderRouter with other components"""
+ 
+     def setUp(self):
+         """Set up the test environment"""
+         # Create mock dependencies
+         self.metrics_manager = MagicMock(spec=MetricsManager)
+         self.exchange_profiler = MagicMock(spec=ExchangeProfiler)
+         self.order_type_optimizer = MagicMock(spec=OrderTypeOptimizer)
+         self.order_book_analyzer = MagicMock(spec=OrderBookAnalyzer)
+         self.liquidity_profiler = MagicMock(spec=LiquidityProfiler)
+         
+         # Setup exchange profiler mocks
+         self.exchange_profiler.get_status.return_value = ExchangeStatus.ONLINE
+         self.exchange_profiler.find_best_exchange.return_value = "binance"
+         self.exchange_profiler.get_reliability_score.return_value = 0.95
+         
+         # Setup exchange capabilities
+         binance_capabilities = MagicMock(spec=ExchangeCapabilities)
+         binance_capabilities.supports_symbol.return_value = True
+         binance_capabilities.has_feature.return_value = True
+         
+         coinbase_capabilities = MagicMock(spec=ExchangeCapabilities)
+         coinbase_capabilities.supports_symbol.return_value = True
+         coinbase_capabilities.has_feature.return_value = True
+         
+         self.exchange_profiler.get_capabilities.side_effect = lambda exchange_id: {
+             "binance": binance_capabilities,
+             "coinbase": coinbase_capabilities

+     def test_multi_exchange_order_routing(self):

+             routing_criteria=RoutingCriteria.HIGHEST_RELIABILITY
+         )
+         
+         # Verify we got allocations for both exchanges
+         self.assertEqual(len(allocations), 2)
+         
+         # Verify allocations sum to the total size
+         total_allocation = sum(allocation["size"] for allocation in allocations)
+         self.assertAlmostEqual(total_allocation, 10.0, places=5)
+         
+         # Verify higher reliability exchange got larger allocation
+         binance_allocation = next(a for a in allocations if a["exchange_id"] == "binance")
+         coinbase_allocation = next(a for a in allocations if a["exchange_id"] == "coinbase")
+         self.assertGreater(binance_allocation["size"], coinbase_allocation["size"])
+         
+         # Check that the metrics were recorded
+         self.metrics_manager.record_metric.assert_called()
+

+ if __name__ == "__main__":
+     unittest.main()

+ class MockOrderBook:
+     """Mock order book data for testing"""
+     
+     def __init__(self, exchange_id, bids=None, asks=None):
+         self.exchange_id = exchange_id
+         self.bids = bids or [
+             [50000.0, 1.5],  # Price, Size
+             [49990.0, 2.0],
+             [49980.0, 3.0],
+             [49970.0, 4.0],
+             [49960.0, 5.0]
+         ]
+         self.asks = asks or [
+             [50010.0, 1.0],
+             [50020.0, 2.0],
+             [50030.0, 3.0],
+             [50040.0, 4.0],
+             [50050.0, 5.0]
+         ]
+         self.timestamp = datetime.utcnow()
+         
+     def get_bids(self, depth=None):
+         """Get bid levels"""
+         if depth:
+             return self.bids[:depth]
+         return self.bids
+     
+     def get_asks(self, depth=None):
+         """Get ask levels"""
+         if depth:
+             return self.asks[:depth]
+         return self.asks
+     
+     def get_mid_price(self):
+         """Get mid price"""
+         best_bid = self.bids[0][0] if self.bids else 0
+         best_ask = self.asks[0][0] if self.asks else 0
+         return (best_bid + best_ask) / 2 if best_bid and best_ask else 0
+     
+     def get_spread(self):
+         """Get bid-ask spread"""
+         best_bid = self.bids[0][0] if self.bids else 0
+         best_ask = self.asks[0][0] if self.asks else 0
+         return best_ask - best_bid if best_bid and best_ask else 0
+

+                 with patch.object(self.price_predictor, 'predict_price_movement',
+                               side_effect=lambda symbol, exchange_id, **kwargs: price_predictions.get(exchange_id, {})):
+                     with patch.object(self.order_flow_analyzer, 'get_order_flow_signal',
+                                   side_effect=lambda symbol, exchange_id: order_flow_signals.get(exchange_id, {})):
+                         
+                         # Test routing for a buy order
+                         buy_decision = self.router.route_order(
+                             symbol=self.test_symbol,
+                             side="buy",
+                             size=1.0,
+                             routing_criteria=RoutingCriteria.SMART,
+                             market_state=market_state
+                         )
+                         
+                         # Test routing for a sell order
+                         sell_decision = self.router.route_order(
+                             symbol=self.test_symbol,
+                             side="sell",
+                             size=1.0,
+                             routing_criteria=RoutingCriteria.SMART,
+                             market_state=market_state
+                         )
+                         
+                         # For smart routing of a buy order, binance should score higher
+                         # due to buy pressure and expected up movement
+                         self.assertEqual(buy_decision.exchange_id, "binance")
+                         
+                         # For smart routing of a sell order, kraken should score higher
+                         # due to expected down movement, which is good for sellers
+                         self.assertEqual(sell_decision.exchange_id, "kraken")
+ 
+ 
+ if __name__ == "__main__":
+     unittest.main() 
```

+             "direction": 1,  # 1 for buy, -1 for sell
+             "strength": 0.8,  # 0-1 scale
+             "timestamp": datetime.utcnow(),
+             "source": "test",
+             "metadata": {
+                 "strategy": self.name,
+                 "reason": "test signal"
+             }
+         }
+         self.signals.append(signal)
+         return [signal]
+     
+     def manage_positions(self, positions, signals):
+         """Manage positions based on signals"""
+         self.positions = positions
+         orders = []
+         
+         for signal in signals:

+             order = {
+                 "symbol": signal["symbol"],
+                 "side": "buy" if signal["direction"] > 0 else "sell",
+                 "type": "market",
+                 "quantity": 1.0 * signal["strength"],
+                 "timestamp": datetime.utcnow(),
+                 "strategy": self.name,
+                 "reason": signal.get("metadata", {}).get("reason", "signal-based")
+             }
+             orders.append(order)
+         
+         self.orders = orders
+         return orders

+ # Instinct AI Trading System Map
+ 
+ This document provides a comprehensive map of all system components, their purpose, locations, and relationships to help with system navigation and understanding.
+ 
+ ## Directory Structure
+ 
+ ```
+ advanced_trading/
+ ├── dashboard/               # Web-based UI for system monitoring and control
+ ├── data/                    # Data acquisition, storage, and management
+ ├── market_microstructure/   # Order book analysis and market microstructure
+ ├── exchange_optimization/   # Exchange-specific optimizations for execution
+ ├── strategies/              # Trading strategies and strategy management
+ ├── observability/           # Metrics, logging, and monitoring
+ ├── backtesting/             # Strategy backtesting and simulation
+ ├── risk/                    # Risk management and position sizing
+ ├── utils/                   # Utility functions and shared components
+ ├── tests/                   # Unit and integration tests
+ ├── models/                  # Machine learning and statistical models
+ ├── examples/                # Example scripts and usage demos
+ ├── docs/                    # Documentation
+ └── assistant/               # AI assistant integration tools
+ ```
+ 
+ ## Core Components
+ 
+ ### 1. Dashboard (`dashboard/`)
+ 
+ **Purpose**: Provides a web-based user interface for monitoring and controlling the system.
+ 
+ **Key Files**:
+ - `dashboard/app.py`: Main dashboard application
+ - `dashboard/components/`: Dashboard UI components
+ - `dashboard/layouts/`: Dashboard page layouts
+ 
+ ### 2. Data Management (`data/`)
+ 
+ **Purpose**: Handles data acquisition, storage, normalization, and delivery to other components.
+ 
+ **Key Files**:
+ - `data/data_manager.py`: Central manager for data operations
+ - `data/exchange_data_provider.py`: Fetches data from exchanges
+ - `data/historical_data.py`: Manages historical data
+ - `data/data_normalizer.py`: Normalizes data from different sources
+ 
+ ### 3. Market Microstructure Analysis (`market_microstructure/`)
+ 
+ **Purpose**: Analyzes order book dynamics and market microstructure to identify trading opportunities and optimize execution.
+ 
+ **Key Files**:
+ - `market_microstructure/analyzers/order_book_analyzer.py`: Analyzes order book patterns
+ - `market_microstructure/analyzers/order_flow_analyzer.py`: Analyzes order flow imbalances
+ - `market_microstructure/analyzers/liquidity_profiler.py`: Profiles liquidity across price levels
+ - `market_microstructure/analyzers/market_impact_model.py`: Estimates market impact of orders
+ - `market_microstructure/analyzers/price_movement_predictor.py`: Predicts short-term price movements
+ 
+ ### 4. Exchange-Specific Optimization (`exchange_optimization/`)
+ 
+ **Purpose**: Improves execution quality by optimizing exchange selection and order parameters.
+ 
+ **Key Files**:
+ - `exchange_optimization/profiles/exchange_profiler.py`: Catalogs exchange capabilities and performance
+ - `exchange_optimization/order_types/order_type_optimizer.py`: Selects optimal order types
+ - `exchange_optimization/routers/smart_order_router.py`: Routes orders to optimal exchanges
+ - `exchange_optimization/analytics/exchange_analytics.py`: Analyzes exchange performance
+ 
+ ### 5. Trading Strategies (`strategies/`)
+ 
+ **Purpose**: Implements various trading strategies and manages their execution.
+ 
+ **Key Files**:
+ - `strategies/strategy_manager.py`: Coordinates strategy execution
+ - `strategies/base_strategy.py`: Base class for all strategies
+ - `strategies/statistical_arbitrage.py`: Statistical arbitrage strategy
+ - `strategies/funding_rate_arbitrage.py`: Funding rate arbitrage strategy
+ - `strategies/microstructure_strategy.py`: Market microstructure-based strategy
+ 
+ ### 6. Observability (`observability/`)
+ 
+ **Purpose**: Provides monitoring, metrics, and logging for system observation.
+ 
+ **Key Files**:
+ - `observability/metrics_manager.py`: Manages system metrics
+ - `observability/logging_manager.py`: Configures structured logging
+ - `observability/trace_manager.py`: Distributed tracing
+ - `observability/alert_manager.py`: Monitoring and alerting
+ 
+ ### 7. Backtesting (`backtesting/`)
+ 
+ **Purpose**: Enables testing and optimization of strategies using historical data.
+ 
+ **Key Files**:
+ - `backtesting/backtest_engine.py`: Core backtesting engine
+ - `backtesting/performance_analyzer.py`: Analyzes strategy performance
+ - `backtesting/optimization_engine.py`: Parameter optimization
+ 
+ ### 8. Risk Management (`risk/`)
+ 
+ **Purpose**: Manages position sizing, risk limits, and portfolio risk.
+ 
+ **Key Files**:
+ - `risk/risk_manager.py`: Central risk management
+ - `risk/position_sizer.py`: Position sizing algorithms
+ - `risk/drawdown_controller.py`: Manages drawdown protection
+ - `risk/exposure_calculator.py`: Calculates exposure metrics
+ 
+ ### 9. Machine Learning Models (`models/`)
+ 
+ **Purpose**: Implements machine learning models for predictions and optimization.
+ 
+ **Key Files**:
+ - `models/model_registry.py`: Manages model versions
+ - `models/feature_engineering.py`: Extracts features for models
+ - `models/training_pipeline.py`: Model training pipeline
+ - `models/inference_engine.py`: Model inference
+ 
+ ## Key Interfaces and Interactions
+ 
+ ### Strategy Execution Flow
+ 
+ ```
+ Strategy Manager → Trading Strategy → Smart Order Router → Exchange Profiler → Exchange API
+ ```
+ 
+ ### Data Flow
+ 
+ ```
+ Exchange API → Data Manager → Market Microstructure → Trading Strategies
+ ```
+ 
+ ### Optimization Flow
+ 
+ ```
+ Order Request → Order Type Optimizer → Smart Order Router → Exchange API
+ ```
+ 
+ ## Common Utilities
+ 
+ ### Data Structures
+ 
+ - `utils/order.py`: Order data structures
+ - `utils/position.py`: Position data structures
+ - `utils/signal.py`: Trading signal data structures
+ 
+ ### Configuration
+ 
+ - `config.py`: System configuration
+ - `utils/config_loader.py`: Configuration management
+ 
+ ### Helpers
+ 
+ - `utils/time_utils.py`: Time-related utilities
+ - `utils/math_utils.py`: Mathematical utilities
+ - `utils/validation.py`: Data validation utilities
+ 
+ ## Testing Framework
+ 
+ - `tests/unit/`: Unit tests for individual components
+ - `tests/integration/`: Integration tests for component interactions
+ - `tests/system/`: End-to-end system tests
+ 
+ ## Documentation
+ 
+ - `docs/SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE.md`: Detailed architecture description
+ - `docs/EXCHANGE_OPTIMIZATION.md`: Exchange optimization documentation
+ - `docs/MARKET_MICROSTRUCTURE.md`: Market microstructure documentation
+ - `docs/API_INTEGRATION.md`: API integration guidelines
+ - `docs/SystemInfo/USERGUIDE`: User guide for system operation
+ 
+ ## Component Dependencies
+ 
+ | Component | Depends On |
+ |-----------|------------|
+ | Trading Strategies | Data Manager, Market Microstructure, Smart Order Router |
+ | Smart Order Router | Exchange Profiler, Order Type Optimizer |
+ | Backtesting Engine | Data Manager, Trading Strategies, Market Simulator |
+ | Dashboard | Metrics Manager, Strategy Manager, Data Manager |
+ | Risk Manager | Position Manager, Trading Strategies |
+ 
+ ## Extension Points
+ 
+ Major extension points for developers:
+ 
+ 1. **New Strategies**: Add new strategy classes in `strategies/`
+ 2. **Custom Order Types**: Add new order types in `exchange_optimization/order_types/`
+ 3. **Exchange Adapters**: Add new exchange connectors in `data/exchanges/`
+ 4. **Risk Models**: Add new risk models in `risk/models/`
+ 5. **Metrics**: Add new metrics in `observability/metrics/`
+ 
+ ## Performance-Critical Components
+ 
+ Components that require special attention for performance:
+ 
+ 1. **Order Book Processing**: `market_microstructure/analyzers/order_book_analyzer.py`
+ 2. **Smart Order Router**: `exchange_optimization/routers/smart_order_router.py`
+ 3. **Real-time Data Processing**: `data/real_time_processor.py`
+ 4. **Signal Generation**: `strategies/*/signal_generator.py` files
+ 
+ ## Configuration Options
+ 
+ Main configuration options by component:
+ 
+ 1. **Data Management**:
+    - Data sources and credentials
+    - Caching and persistence policies
+    - Update intervals
+ 
+ 2. **Trading Strategies**:
+    - Strategy-specific parameters
+    - Symbols and timeframes
+    - Entry/exit conditions
+ 
+ 3. **Exchange Optimization**:
+    - Routing preferences
+    - Order type selection criteria
+    - Performance thresholds
+ 
+ 4. **Risk Management**:
+    - Position sizing rules
+    - Drawdown limits
+    - Exposure constraints
+ 
+ ## Implementation Status
+ 
+ Current implementation status of major components:
+ 
+ | Component | Status | Notes |
+ |-----------|--------|-------|
+ | Data Manager | Complete | Supporting major exchanges |
+ | Market Microstructure | Complete | All analyzers implemented |
+ | Exchange Optimization | Complete | Core functionality implemented |
+ | Trading Strategies | In Progress | Core strategies implemented |
+ | Backtesting Framework | Complete | Supporting all strategies |
+ | Risk Management | Complete | Core risk controls implemented |
+ | Dashboard | In Progress | Basic monitoring implemented |
+ | ML Models | Planned | Initial research done |
+ 
+ ## Future Development
+ 
+ Upcoming development areas:
+ 
+ 1. **ML-Based Signal Generation**: Enhance strategies with ML predictions
+ 2. **Advanced Execution Algorithms**: Add TWAP, VWAP, and adaptive algorithms
+ 3. **Cross-Exchange Arbitrage**: Improve multi-exchange strategies
+ 4. **Performance Optimization**: Optimize critical paths for lower latency
+ 5. **Extended Exchange Support**: Add more exchanges and asset classes 
```
