+ """
+ Component Registry
+ 
+ This module provides a programmatic way to discover and register system
+ components, their locations, and metadata. It serves as a central registry
+ for all major components in the system, enabling dynamic discovery, 
+ documentation, and dependency management.
+ """
+ 
+ import logging
+ import importlib
+ import inspect
+ from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Callable, Type, Set
+ from enum import Enum
+ from dataclasses import dataclass
+ 
+ logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
+ 
+ class ComponentType(Enum):
+     """Types of system components"""
+     STRATEGY = "strategy"
+     ANALYZER = "analyzer"
+     OPTIMIZER = "optimizer"
+     ROUTER = "router"
+     DATA_PROVIDER = "data_provider"
+     RISK_MODEL = "risk_model"
+     METRIC = "metric"
+     UTILITY = "utility"
+     EXCHANGE = "exchange"
+ 
+ 
+ @dataclass
+ class ComponentInfo:
+     """Information about a system component"""
+     name: str
+     type: ComponentType
+     module_path: str
+     class_name: str
+     description: str
+     dependencies: List[str] = None
+     tags: List[str] = None
+     status: str = "active"
+     maintainer: str = ""
+     version: str = "1.0.0"
+     
+     def __post_init__(self):
+         if self.dependencies is None:
+             self.dependencies = []
+         if self.tags is None:
+             self.tags = []
+     
+     def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+         """Convert component info to dictionary"""
+         return {
+             "name": self.name,
+             "type": self.type.value,
+             "module_path": self.module_path,
+             "class_name": self.class_name,
+             "description": self.description,
+             "dependencies": self.dependencies,
+             "tags": self.tags,
+             "status": self.status,
+             "maintainer": self.maintainer,
+             "version": self.version
+         }
+ 
+ 
+ class ComponentRegistry:
+     """Registry for system components"""
+     
+     def __init__(self):
+         """Initialize the component registry"""
+         self.components: Dict[str, ComponentInfo] = {}
+         self.component_instances: Dict[str, Any] = {}
+         self._initialize_registry()
+     
+     def _initialize_registry(self):
+         """Initialize the registry with core components"""
+         # Initialize with core components
+         self.register_component(
+             ComponentInfo(
+                 name="exchange_profiler",
+                 type=ComponentType.ANALYZER,
+                 module_path="advanced_trading.exchange_optimization.profiles.exchange_profiler",
+                 class_name="ExchangeProfiler",
+                 description="Catalogs exchange capabilities and tracks performance metrics",
+                 dependencies=["metrics_manager"],
+                 tags=["exchange", "optimization", "profiling"]
+             )
+         )
+         
+         self.register_component(
+             ComponentInfo(
+                 name="order_type_optimizer",
+                 type=ComponentType.OPTIMIZER,
+                 module_path="advanced_trading.exchange_optimization.order_types.order_type_optimizer",
+                 class_name="OrderTypeOptimizer",
+                 description="Selects optimal order types based on market conditions and goals",
+                 dependencies=["exchange_profiler", "metrics_manager"],
+                 tags=["exchange", "optimization", "order_type"]
+             )
+         )
+         
+         self.register_component(
+             ComponentInfo(
+                 name="smart_order_router",
+                 type=ComponentType.ROUTER,
+                 module_path="advanced_trading.exchange_optimization.routers.smart_order_router",
+                 class_name="SmartOrderRouter",
+                 description="Routes orders to optimal exchanges based on criteria",
+                 dependencies=["exchange_profiler", "order_type_optimizer", "metrics_manager"],
+                 tags=["exchange", "optimization", "routing"]
+             )
+         )
+         
+         self.register_component(
+             ComponentInfo(
+                 name="exchange_analytics",
+                 type=ComponentType.ANALYZER,
+                 module_path="advanced_trading.exchange_optimization.analytics.exchange_analytics",
+                 class_name="ExchangeAnalytics",
+                 description="Measures and reports on exchange performance",
+                 dependencies=["metrics_manager"],
+                 tags=["exchange", "optimization", "analytics"]
+             )
+         )
+         
+         self.register_component(
+             ComponentInfo(
+                 name="order_book_analyzer",
+                 type=ComponentType.ANALYZER,
+                 module_path="advanced_trading.market_microstructure.analyzers.order_book_analyzer",
+                 class_name="OrderBookAnalyzer",
+                 description="Analyzes order book data to extract insights",
+                 dependencies=["metrics_manager"],
+                 tags=["market_microstructure", "order_book"]
+             )
+         )
+         
+         self.register_component(
+             ComponentInfo(
+                 name="liquidity_profiler",
+                 type=ComponentType.ANALYZER,
+                 module_path="advanced_trading.market_microstructure.analyzers.liquidity_profiler",
+                 class_name="LiquidityProfiler",
+                 description="Analyzes market liquidity across price levels",
+                 dependencies=["metrics_manager"],
+                 tags=["market_microstructure", "liquidity"]
+             )
+         )
+         
+         self.register_component(
+             ComponentInfo(
+                 name="order_flow_analyzer",
+                 type=ComponentType.ANALYZER,
+                 module_path="advanced_trading.market_microstructure.analyzers.order_flow_analyzer",
+                 class_name="OrderFlowAnalyzer",
+                 description="Detects order flow imbalances and predicts short-term movements",
+                 dependencies=["metrics_manager"],
+                 tags=["market_microstructure", "order_flow"]
+             )
+         )
+         
+         self.register_component(
+             ComponentInfo(
+                 name="price_movement_predictor",
+                 type=ComponentType.ANALYZER,
+                 module_path="advanced_trading.market_microstructure.analyzers.price_movement_predictor",
+                 class_name="PriceMovementPredictor",
+                 description="Predicts short-term price movements based on microstructure",
+                 dependencies=["metrics_manager", "order_book_analyzer", "order_flow_analyzer"],
+                 tags=["market_microstructure", "prediction"]
+             )
+         )
+         
+         self.register_component(
+             ComponentInfo(
+                 name="strategy_manager",
+                 type=ComponentType.STRATEGY,
+                 module_path="advanced_trading.strategies.strategy_manager",
+                 class_name="StrategyManager",
+                 description="Coordinates the execution of trading strategies",
+                 dependencies=["metrics_manager", "smart_order_router"],
+                 tags=["strategy", "execution"]
+             )
+         )
+         
+         self.register_component(
+             ComponentInfo(
+                 name="backtest_engine",
+                 type=ComponentType.UTILITY,
+                 module_path="advanced_trading.backtesting.backtest_engine",
+                 class_name="BacktestEngine",
+                 description="Engine for backtesting trading strategies",
+                 dependencies=["data_manager"],
+                 tags=["backtesting", "simulation"]
+             )
+         )
+         
+         self.register_component(
+             ComponentInfo(
+                 name="risk_manager",
+                 type=ComponentType.RISK_MODEL,
+                 module_path="advanced_trading.risk.risk_manager",
+                 class_name="RiskManager",
+                 description="Manages trading risk and position sizing",
+                 dependencies=["metrics_manager"],
+                 tags=["risk", "position_sizing"]
+             )
+         )
+         
+         self.register_component(
+             ComponentInfo(
+                 name="metrics_manager",
+                 type=ComponentType.UTILITY,
+                 module_path="advanced_trading.observability.metrics_manager",
+                 class_name="MetricsManager",
+                 description="Manages system metrics and monitoring",
+                 dependencies=[],
+                 tags=["observability", "metrics"]
+             )
+         )
+         
+         self.register_component(
+             ComponentInfo(
+                 name="data_manager",
+                 type=ComponentType.DATA_PROVIDER,
+                 module_path="advanced_trading.data.data_manager",
+                 class_name="DataManager",
+                 description="Centralizes data acquisition and management",
+                 dependencies=["metrics_manager"],
+                 tags=["data", "exchange_integration"]
+             )
+         )
+     
+     def register_component(self, component_info: ComponentInfo) -> None:
+         """
+         Register a component with the registry.
+         
+         Args:
+             component_info: Information about the component
+         """
+         if component_info.name in self.components:
+             logger.warning(f"Component {component_info.name} already registered, updating")
+         
+         self.components[component_info.name] = component_info
+         logger.info(f"Registered component: {component_info.name} ({component_info.class_name})")
+     
+     def unregister_component(self, name: str) -> bool:
+         """
+         Unregister a component from the registry.
+         
+         Args:
+             name: Name of the component to unregister
+             
+         Returns:
+             True if successful, False if component not found
+         """
+         if name in self.components:
+             del self.components[name]
+             if name in self.component_instances:
+                 del self.component_instances[name]
+             logger.info(f"Unregistered component: {name}")
+             return True
+         
+         logger.warning(f"Component {name} not found in registry")
+         return False
+     
+     def get_component_info(self, name: str) -> Optional[ComponentInfo]:
+         """
+         Get information about a registered component.
+         
+         Args:
+             name: Name of the component
+             
+         Returns:
+             ComponentInfo or None if not found
+         """
+         return self.components.get(name)
+     
+     def get_components_by_type(self, component_type: ComponentType) -> List[ComponentInfo]:
+         """
+         Get all components of a specific type.
+         
+         Args:
+             component_type: Type of components to retrieve
+             
+         Returns:
+             List of ComponentInfo objects
+         """
+         return [
+             comp for comp in self.components.values() 
+             if comp.type == component_type
+         ]
+     
+     def get_components_by_tag(self, tag: str) -> List[ComponentInfo]:
+         """
+         Get all components with a specific tag.
+         
+         Args:
+             tag: Tag to filter by
+             
+         Returns:
+             List of ComponentInfo objects
+         """
+         return [
+             comp for comp in self.components.values() 
+             if tag in comp.tags
+         ]
+     
+     def get_component_instance(self, name: str, **kwargs) -> Any:
+         """
+         Get an instance of a component, creating it if needed.
+         
+         Args:
+             name: Name of the component
+             **kwargs: Parameters to pass to the component constructor
+             
+         Returns:
+             Component instance or None if not found
+         """
+         # Return existing instance if available
+         if name in self.component_instances:
+             return self.component_instances[name]
+         
+         # Get component info
+         component_info = self.get_component_info(name)
+         if not component_info:
+             logger.error(f"Component {name} not found in registry")
+             return None
+         
+         try:
+             # Import the module
+             module = importlib.import_module(component_info.module_path)
+             
+             # Get the class
+             component_class = getattr(module, component_info.class_name)
+             
+             # Create an instance
+             instance = component_class(**kwargs)
+             
+             # Store the instance
+             self.component_instances[name] = instance
+             
+             return instance
+         except (ImportError, AttributeError, TypeError) as e:
+             logger.error(f"Error instantiating component {name}: {e}")
+             return None
+     
+     def get_all_components(self) -> List[ComponentInfo]:
+         """
+         Get information about all registered components.
+         
+         Returns:
+             List of all ComponentInfo objects
+         """
+         return list(self.components.values())
+     
+     def get_component_dependencies(self, name: str) -> List[ComponentInfo]:
+         """
+         Get all dependencies of a component.
+         
+         Args:
+             name: Name of the component
+             
+         Returns:
+             List of ComponentInfo objects for dependencies
+         """
+         component = self.get_component_info(name)
+         if not component:
+             return []
+         
+         dependencies = []
+         for dep_name in component.dependencies:
+             dep_info = self.get_component_info(dep_name)
+             if dep_info:
+                 dependencies.append(dep_info)
+             else:
+                 logger.warning(f"Dependency {dep_name} not found for component {name}")
+         
+         return dependencies
+     
+     def get_dependent_components(self, name: str) -> List[ComponentInfo]:
+         """
+         Get all components that depend on this component.
+         
+         Args:
+             name: Name of the component
+             
+         Returns:
+             List of ComponentInfo objects that depend on this component
+         """
+         dependents = []
+         for component in self.components.values():
+             if name in component.dependencies:
+                 dependents.append(component)
+         
+         return dependents
+     
+     def check_dependencies(self, name: str) -> List[str]:
+         """
+         Check if all dependencies of a component are registered.
+         
+         Args:
+             name: Name of the component
+             
+         Returns:
+             List of missing dependencies
+         """
+         component = self.get_component_info(name)
+         if not component:
+             return []
+         
+         missing = []
+         for dep_name in component.dependencies:
+             if dep_name not in self.components:
+                 missing.append(dep_name)
+         
+         return missing
+     
+     def get_dependency_graph(self) -> Dict[str, List[str]]:
+         """
+         Get a dependency graph of all components.
+         
+         Returns:
+             Dictionary mapping component names to lists of dependency names
+         """
+         graph = {}
+         for name, component in self.components.items():
+             graph[name] = component.dependencies
+         
+         return graph
+     
+     def get_component_docs(self, name: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+         """
+         Get documentation about a component, including its class docs.
+         
+         Args:
+             name: Name of the component
+             
+         Returns:
+             Dictionary with component documentation
+         """
+         component = self.get_component_info(name)
+         if not component:
+             return {}
+         
+         try:
+             module = importlib.import_module(component.module_path)
+             component_class = getattr(module, component.class_name)
+             
+             # Get class docstring
+             class_doc = inspect.getdoc(component_class) or ""
+             
+             # Get method info
+             methods = {}
+             for method_name, method in inspect.getmembers(component_class, inspect.isfunction):
+                 if not method_name.startswith('_'):  # Skip private methods
+                     methods[method_name] = inspect.getdoc(method) or ""
+             
+             # Get initialization parameters
+             init_signature = inspect.signature(component_class.__init__)
+             params = {
+                 name: {
+                     "annotation": str(param.annotation),
+                     "default": str(param.default) if param.default is not param.empty else None,
+                     "required": param.default is param.empty and name != 'self'
+                 }
+                 for name, param in init_signature.parameters.items()
+             }
+             
+             return {
+                 "info": component.to_dict(),
+                 "class_doc": class_doc,
+                 "methods": methods,
+                 "parameters": params,
+                 "dependencies": [dep.name for dep in self.get_component_dependencies(name)]
+             }
+             
+         except (ImportError, AttributeError) as e:
+             logger.error(f"Error getting docs for component {name}: {e}")
+             return {"info": component.to_dict(), "error": str(e)}
+ 
+ 
+ # Singleton instance
+ _registry = ComponentRegistry()
+ 
+ # Export public functions
+ get_component_info = _registry.get_component_info
+ get_components_by_type = _registry.get_components_by_type
+ get_components_by_tag = _registry.get_components_by_tag
+ get_component_instance = _registry.get_component_instance
+ get_all_components = _registry.get_all_components
+ register_component = _registry.register_component
+ unregister_component = _registry.unregister_component
+ get_component_dependencies = _registry.get_component_dependencies
+ get_dependent_components = _registry.get_dependent_components
+ check_dependencies = _registry.check_dependencies
+ get_dependency_graph = _registry.get_dependency_graph
+ get_component_docs = _registry.get_component_docs
+ 
+ def list_available_components() -> Dict[str, List[str]]:
+     """
+     Get a list of all available components grouped by type.
+     
+     Returns:
+         Dictionary mapping component types to lists of component names
+     """
+     result = {}
+     for comp_type in ComponentType:
+         components = get_components_by_type(comp_type)
+         result[comp_type.value] = [comp.name for comp in components]
+     
+     return result
+ 
+ def get_component_details() -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
+     """
+     Get detailed information about all components.
+     
+     Returns:
+         Dictionary mapping component names to their details
+     """
+     return {
+         comp.name: comp.to_dict() 
+         for comp in get_all_components()
+     } 
```

Let's create a script to generate a system visualization based on the component registry:

```diff
- 
+ #!/usr/bin/env python3
+ """
+ System Visualization Tool
+ 
+ Generates visual representations of system components and their dependencies
+ for better system understanding and documentation.
+ """
+ 
+ import os
+ import sys
+ import argparse
+ import logging
+ from typing import Dict, List, Set, Tuple, Optional
+ 
+ # Add the parent directory to the path
+ sys.path.insert(0, os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), '..')))
+ 
+ from utils.component_registry import (
+     get_all_components, 
+     get_component_info,
+     get_dependency_graph,
+     ComponentType,
+     get_components_by_type
+ )
+ 
+ # Configure logging
+ logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format='%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s')
+ logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
+ 
+ 
+ def generate_mermaid_diagram(output_path: str = None) -> str:
+     """
+     Generate a Mermaid.js component diagram.
+     
+     Args:
+         output_path: Optional path to save the diagram
+         
+     Returns:
+         Mermaid diagram as string
+     """
+     # Get all components
+     components = get_all_components()
+     
+     # Get dependency graph
+     dep_graph = get_dependency_graph()
+     
+     # Start Mermaid diagram
+     mermaid = "```mermaid\ngraph TD\n"
+     
+     # Component style definitions
+     style_defs = {
+         ComponentType.STRATEGY: "fill:#f9d5e5,stroke:#333,stroke-width:1px",
+         ComponentType.ANALYZER: "fill:#b5ead7,stroke:#333,stroke-width:1px",
+         ComponentType.OPTIMIZER: "fill:#c7ceea,stroke:#333,stroke-width:1px",
+         ComponentType.ROUTER: "fill:#ffd6a5,stroke:#333,stroke-width:1px",
+         ComponentType.DATA_PROVIDER: "fill:#cbf0f8,stroke:#333,stroke-width:1px",
+         ComponentType.RISK_MODEL: "fill:#ff9aa2,stroke:#333,stroke-width:1px",
+         ComponentType.METRIC: "fill:#cca8e9,stroke:#333,stroke-width:1px",
+         ComponentType.UTILITY: "fill:#e2f0cb,stroke:#333,stroke-width:1px",
+         ComponentType.EXCHANGE: "fill:#d0e0fb,stroke:#333,stroke-width:1px"
+     }
+     
+     # Add all components to the diagram
+     for component in components:
+         mermaid += f"    {component.name}[{component.name}]\n"
+     
+     # Add dependencies
+     for component_name, dependencies in dep_graph.items():
+         for dep in dependencies:
+             mermaid += f"    {dep} --> {component_name}\n"
+     
+     # Add styling
+     for component in components:
+         style = style_defs.get(component.type, "fill:#f9f9f9,stroke:#333,stroke-width:1px")
+         mermaid += f"    style {component.name} {style}\n"
+     
+     # Add legend
+     mermaid += "    subgraph Legend\n"
+     for comp_type, style in style_defs.items():
+         mermaid += f"    legend_{comp_type.value}[{comp_type.value}]\n"
+         mermaid += f"    style legend_{comp_type.value} {style}\n"
+     mermaid += "    end\n"
+     
+     # End diagram
+     mermaid += "```\n"
+     
+     # Save to file if requested
+     if output_path:
+         with open(output_path, 'w') as f:
+             f.write(mermaid)
+         logger.info(f"Mermaid diagram saved to {output_path}")
+     
+     return mermaid
+ 
+ 
+ def generate_dot_graph(output_path: str = None) -> str:
+     """
+     Generate a DOT graph for use with Graphviz.
+     
+     Args:
+         output_path: Optional path to save the DOT file
+         
+     Returns:
+         DOT graph as string
+     """
+     # Get all components
+     components = get_all_components()
+     
+     # Get dependency graph
+     dep_graph = get_dependency_graph()
+     
+     # Start DOT graph
+     dot = "digraph InstinctAISystem {\n"
+     dot += "    rankdir=TB;\n"
+     dot += "    node [shape=box, style=filled, fontname=Arial];\n"
+     dot += "    edge [fontname=Arial];\n"
+     dot += "    compound=true;\n\n"
+     
+     # Colors for component types
+     colors = {
+         ComponentType.STRATEGY: "#f9d5e5",
+         ComponentType.ANALYZER: "#b5ead7",
+         ComponentType.OPTIMIZER: "#c7ceea",
+         ComponentType.ROUTER: "#ffd6a5",
+         ComponentType.DATA_PROVIDER: "#cbf0f8",
+         ComponentType.RISK_MODEL: "#ff9aa2",
+         ComponentType.METRIC: "#cca8e9",
+         ComponentType.UTILITY: "#e2f0cb",
+         ComponentType.EXCHANGE: "#d0e0fb"
+     }
+     
+     # Group components by type
+     comp_by_type = {}
+     for comp_type in ComponentType:
+         comp_by_type[comp_type] = []
+     
+     for component in components:
+         comp_by_type[component.type].append(component)
+     
+     # Create clusters for each component type
+     for comp_type, comps in comp_by_type.items():
+         if not comps:
+             continue
+             
+         # Create subgraph
+         dot += f"    subgraph cluster_{comp_type.value} {{\n"
+         dot += f"        label=\"{comp_type.value}\";\n"
+         dot += f"        style=filled;\n"
+         dot += f"        color=\"{colors.get(comp_type, '#f9f9f9')}\";\n"
+         dot += f"        node [style=filled, color=white];\n"
+         
+         # Add components to subgraph
+         for component in comps:
+             dot += f"        {component.name} [label=\"{component.name}\"];\n"
+             
+         dot += "    }\n\n"
+     
+     # Add dependencies
+     for component_name, dependencies in dep_graph.items():
+         for dep in dependencies:
+             dot += f"    {dep} -> {component_name};\n"
+     
+     # End graph
+     dot += "}\n"
+     
+     # Save to file if requested
+     if output_path:
+         with open(output_path, 'w') as f:
+             f.write(dot)
+         logger.info(f"DOT graph saved to {output_path}")
+     
+     return dot
+ 
+ 
+ def generate_ascii_graph(output_path: str = None) -> str:
+     """
+     Generate an ASCII representation of the component graph.
+     
+     Args:
+         output_path: Optional path to save the ASCII graph
+         
+     Returns:
+         ASCII graph as string
+     """
+     # Get all components
+     components = get_all_components()
+     
+     # Get dependency graph
+     dep_graph = get_dependency_graph()
+     
+     # Compute component levels (distance from root)
+     levels = compute_component_levels(dep_graph)
+     
+     # Sort components by level
+     level_components = {}
+     for component in components:
+         level = levels.get(component.name, 0)
+         if level not in level_components:
+             level_components[level] = []
+         level_components[level].append(component)
+     
+     # Generate ASCII representation
+     max_level = max(levels.values()) if levels else 0
+     ascii_graph = ["Component Dependency Graph:", ""]
+     
+     for level in range(max_level + 1):
+         comps = level_components.get(level, [])
+         if not comps:
+             continue
+             
+         # Add level header
+         ascii_graph.append(f"Level {level}:")
+         
+         # Add components
+         for component in sorted(comps, key=lambda x: x.name):
+             deps = dep_graph.get(component.name, [])
+             dep_str = ", ".join(deps) if deps else "none"
+             ascii_graph.append(f"  [{component.type.value}] {component.name} (deps: {dep_str})")
+         
+         ascii_graph.append("")
+     
+     # Add legend
+     ascii_graph.append("Component Types:")
+     for comp_type in ComponentType:
+         ascii_graph.append(f"  [{comp_type.value}]: {comp_type.name}")
+     
+     # Join lines
+     result = "\n".join(ascii_graph)
+     
+     # Save to file if requested
+     if output_path:
+         with open(output_path, 'w') as f:
+             f.write(result)
+         logger.info(f"ASCII graph saved to {output_path}")
+     
+     return result
+ 
+ 
+ def compute_component_levels(dep_graph: Dict[str, List[str]]) -> Dict[str, int]:
+     """
+     Compute the dependency level of each component.
+     
+     Args:
+         dep_graph: Dependency graph
+         
+     Returns:
+         Dictionary mapping component names to their levels
+     """
+     # Find root components (no dependencies)
+     root_components = set()
+     all_components = set(dep_graph.keys())
+     
+     for component, deps in dep_graph.items():
+         if not deps:
+             root_components.add(component)
+     
+     # Handle circular dependencies
+     if not root_components:
+         # Find component with fewest dependencies
+         min_deps = float('inf')
+         min_comp = None
+         
+         for component, deps in dep_graph.items():
+             if len(deps) < min_deps:
+                 min_deps = len(deps)
+                 min_comp = component
+         
+         if min_comp:
+             root_components.add(min_comp)
+     
+     # Compute levels with BFS
+     levels = {comp: 0 for comp in root_components}
+     visited = set(root_components)
+     queue = list(root_components)
+     
+     while queue:
+         current = queue.pop(0)
+         current_level = levels[current]
+         
+         # Find components that depend on current
+         dependents = []
+         for component, deps in dep_graph.items():
+             if current in deps and component not in visited:
+                 dependents.append(component)
+         
+         # Update levels and queue
+         for dependent in dependents:
+             levels[dependent] = current_level + 1
+             visited.add(dependent)
+             queue.append(dependent)
+     
+     # Handle unvisited components (isolated or part of cycles)
+     unvisited = all_components - visited
+     while unvisited:
+         # Find component with at least one visited dependency
+         found = False
+         for component in unvisited:
+             deps = dep_graph.get(component, [])
+             visited_deps = [dep for dep in deps if dep in visited]
+             
+             if visited_deps:
+                 # Set level to max level of dependencies + 1
+                 max_dep_level = max(levels.get(dep, 0) for dep in visited_deps)
+                 levels[component] = max_dep_level + 1
+                 visited.add(component)
+                 unvisited.remove(component)
+                 found = True
+                 break
+         
+         # If no component could be assigned a level, assign level 0 to one
+         if not found and unvisited:
+             component = next(iter(unvisited))
+             levels[component] = 0
+             visited.add(component)
+             unvisited.remove(component)
+     
+     return levels
+ 
+ 
+ def generate_component_list(output_path: str = None) -> str:
+     """
+     Generate a markdown component list grouped by type.
+     
+     Args:
+         output_path: Optional path to save the component list
+         
+     Returns:
+         Markdown component list as string
+     """
+     markdown = "# Instinct AI Trading System Components\n\n"
+     
+     # Group components by type
+     for comp_type in ComponentType:
+         components = get_components_by_type(comp_type)
+         if not components:
+             continue
+             
+         markdown += f"## {comp_type.value.capitalize()} Components\n\n"
+         
+         # Add component table
+         markdown += "| Component | Description | Dependencies |\n"
+         markdown += "|-----------|-------------|-------------|\n"
+         
+         for component in sorted(components, key=lambda x: x.name):
+             deps = ", ".join(component.dependencies) if component.dependencies else "-"
+             markdown += f"| {component.name} | {component.description} | {deps} |\n"
+         
+         markdown += "\n"
+     
+     # Save to file if requested
+     if output_path:
+         with open(output_path, 'w') as f:
+             f.write(markdown)
+         logger.info(f"Component list saved to {output_path}")
+     
+     return markdown
+ 
+ 
+ if __name__ == "__main__":
+     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Generate system visualization")
+     parser.add_argument(
+         "--format", 
+         choices=["mermaid", "dot", "ascii", "markdown"],
+         default="mermaid",
+         help="Output format (default: mermaid)"
+     )
+     parser.add_argument(
+         "--output", 
+         help="Output file path"
+     )
+     
+     args = parser.parse_args()
+     
+     # Generate visualization based on format
+     if args.format == "mermaid":
+         result = generate_mermaid_diagram(args.output)
+     elif args.format == "dot":
+         result = generate_dot_graph(args.output)
+     elif args.format == "ascii":
+         result = generate_ascii_graph(args.output)
+     elif args.format == "markdown":
+         result = generate_component_list(args.output)
+     
+     # Print result if no output file specified
+     if not args.output:
+         print(result) 
```
